   362	// Each original adoption cohort saw one snapshot. Re-entry restores that same
   363	// view across all its declarations; later cohorts read earlier cohorts' outputs.
   364	fn restore_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   365	    tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=(SELECT o.data FROM temp._backfill_originals o WHERE o.t=r.t AND o.id=r.id) WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM temp._backfill_originals o WHERE o.t=r.t AND o.id=r.id)",[])?;Ok(())
   366	}
   367	// A reversed, originally absent cell must have the same meaning in explicit
   368	// arguments, point lookups and lookup indexes. Null is a temporary source-view
   369	// marker; the saved physical image is restored before committing any patch.
   370	fn mask_unprovided(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   371	    let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT r.t,r.id,octet_length(r.data) FROM r JOIN temp._backfill_unprovided m ON m.t=r.t AND m.id=r.id ORDER BY r.t,r.id")?;
   372	    let mut rows=q.query([])?;
   373	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   374	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;
   375	        let mut meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);meter.heap((r.get::<_,i64>(2)?.max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
   376	        let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;
   377	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
   378	        if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut row {
   379	            let mut columns=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT column_name FROM temp._backfill_unprovided WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?;
   380	            for c in columns.query_map(params![t,id],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))? {fields.entry(c?).or_insert(Value::Null);}
   381	        }
   382	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   383	    }Ok(())
   384	}
   385	// Recorded later cohorts originally saw earlier releases' completed patches.
   386	// Replaying those older cohorts virtually preserves that ordering; fresh
   387	// declarations still run first against the untouched predecessor snapshot.
   388	fn replay_earlier_cohorts(tx:&Tx<'_>,epoch:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   389	    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,octet_length(patch),patch FROM temp._backfill_pending WHERE CAST(json_extract(evidence,'$.epoch') AS INTEGER)<?1 ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
   390	    let mut rows=stmt.query([epoch])?;
   391	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   392	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;
   393	        let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   394	        let Some(bytes)=bytes else{continue};
   395	        let mut admission=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);admission.heap((bytes.max(0) as u64).saturating_add(r.get::<_,i64>(2)?.max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
   396	        let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;
   397	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
   398	        let patch:BTreeMap<String,Value>=decode(&r.get::<_,String>(3)?)?;
   399	        if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut row {fields.extend(patch);}
   400	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   401	    }Ok(())
   402	}

   404	fn historical_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>,epoch:i64,keys:&BTreeSet<String>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   405	    let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=1 AND epoch=?1 ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
   406	    let mut rows=q.query([epoch])?;
   407	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   408	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;let key:String=r.get(2)?;if !keys.contains(&key){continue;}
   409	        let data:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   410	        let Some(data)=data else {continue};
   411	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
   412	        let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;let e=evidence(tx,t,&id,&key)?.unwrap();
   413	        let fields=match &mut row {Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f)=>f,_=>return Err(refusal("invalid historical row"))};
   414	        if let Value::Row(inputs)|Value::Map(inputs)=&e.inputs {for(c,value)in inputs {
   415	            if e.absent.contains(c) || !fields.contains_key(c) || fields.get(c)==Some(value) {
   416	                let invalid:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND invalid=1)",params![t,id,input_ref(&key,c)],|r|r.get(0))?;
   417	                if invalid {return Err(refusal(format!("historical backfill reference {c} on {id} lost its parent lifetime")));}
   418	                if e.absent.contains(c) {fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}else{fields.entry(c.clone()).or_insert(value.clone());}
   419	            }
   420	        }}
   421	        for c in e.after.keys() {if !e.before.contains_key(c) {fields.insert(c.clone(),Value::Null);}}
   422	        for(c,value)in e.before {fields.insert(c,value);}
   423	        // Keep the receipt's omitted-input meaning separate from its later
   424	        // rollback output preimage/default.
   425	        for c in &e.absent {if let Ok(value)=e.inputs.field(c){fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}}
   426	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   427	    } Ok(())
   428	}
   429	fn rebuild_reads(tx:&Tx<'_>,source:&Schema,reads:&BTreeSet<(String,String)>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   430	    for (name,index) in reads {
   431	        let old=&source.tables[name].indexes[index];let ti=tx.num(&format!("{name}.{index}"))?;
   432	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[ti])?;
   433	        super::activation::walk(tx,tx.num(name)?,|id,mut row|{
   434	            materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,&source.tables[name]);
   435	            for key in index_keys(old,&row,id){tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO i(ti,k,id) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![ti,key,id])?;}Ok(())
   436	        })?;
   437	        if old.unique {validate_lookup(tx,ti,old.components.len(),name,index)?;}
   438	    } Ok(())
   439	}

   569	pub(super) fn retire_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,previous:Option<&Schema>,now:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   570	    let mut source=previous.cloned().unwrap_or_default();source.tables=sources.clone();source.backfills.clear();source.prepare_receipt_dependencies();
   571	    let names:Vec<String>=source.through_order().into_iter().rev().filter(|n|source.tables[n].ephemeral.is_none() && source.maintain_for(n).is_none()).collect();
   572	    let mut due=false;
   573	    for name in &names {
   574	        let t=tx.num(name)?;
   575	        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,row|{if !due {due=source_expired(tx,t,id,&source.tables[name],&row,now)?;}Ok(())})?;
   576	    }
   577	    if !due{return Ok(())}
   578	    // Restored sources have no live index entries. Every reference action,
   579	    // maintained dependency and retained-child probe uses this assembled view.
   580	    for name in &names {
   581	        let table=&source.tables[name];
   582	        for (index,definition) in &table.indexes {
   583	            let ti=tx.num(&format!("{name}.{index}"))?;tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[ti])?;
   584	            let work=crate::rows::adoption::Work::table(table);
   585	            let scratch=|row:&Value|crate::rows::adoption::contribution_bytes(row,table);
   586	            super::activation_walk::walk_metered(tx,tx.num(name)?,work,if work.index_entries!=0 {Some(&scratch)}else{None},|id,mut row,_|{
   587	                materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,table);
   588	                for key in index_keys(definition,&row,id){tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO i(ti,k,id) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![ti,key,id])?;}Ok(())
   589	            })?;
   590	        }
   591	    }
   592	    if tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_cap_counts')",[],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {
   593	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _cap_counts",[])?;
   594	        for name in &names {let table=&source.tables[name];super::activation::walk(tx,tx.num(name)?,|_,row|tx.update_cap_counts(name,table,None,Some(&row)).map(|_|()))?;}
   595	    }
   596	    validate_action_bounds(tx,&source,now)?;
   597	    tx.clear_activation_scan();
   598	    for name in &names {
   599	        let t=tx.num(name)?;
   600	        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,_|{
   601	            let Some(mut row)=tx.get(name,id)? else{return Ok(())};
   602	            if !source_expired(tx,t,id,&source.tables[name],&row,now)? {return Ok(())}
   603	            materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,&source.tables[name]);
   604	            let mut runner=Runner::new(tx,&source,Context::system().at(now),Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort),tx.next_seq()?);
   605	            match runner.retire_expired_row(name,&row) {
   606	                Ok(true)=>{tx.set_meta("activation_population","true")?;},
   607	                Ok(false)=>return Err(refusal(format!("source expiry {name}/{id} is blocked by retained children; resolve them in a bridge before adoption"))),
   608	                Err(e)=>return Err(refusal(format!("source expiry {name}/{id}: {e}"))),
   609	            }Ok(())
   610	        })?;
   611	    }
   612	    for name in &names {
   613	        let cleared:Vec<_>=source.tables[name].on_delete.iter().filter(|(_,a)|**a==crate::schema::references::OnDelete::SetNull).map(|(c,_)|c.clone()).collect();
   614	        if cleared.is_empty(){continue;}
   615	        super::activation::walk(tx,tx.num(name)?,|id,row|{
   616	            for c in &cleared {if row.field(c).is_ok_and(|v|*v==Value::Null){
   617	                let t=tx.num(name)?;
   618	                tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_values SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3 AND column_name=?4",params![canonical_json(&Value::Null),t,id,c])?;

   629	fn source_expired(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,table:&Table,row:&Value,now:i64)->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{
   630	    if table.expired(row,now){return Ok(true)}
   631	    let definition:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT definition FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   632	    if let Some(definition)=definition {let old:Table=decode(&definition)?;return Ok(old.expired(row,now));}Ok(false)
   633	}
   634	
   635	fn validate_action_bounds(tx:&Tx<'_>,source:&Schema,now:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   636	    for parent in source.tables.keys() {for r in source.referencing(parent).into_iter().filter(|r|r.action.writes()) {
   637	        let bound=source.reference_cap(r.child,r.column).ok_or_else(||refusal("historical reference action has no bound"))?;
   638	        let index=source.reference_index(r.child,r.column).ok_or_else(||refusal("historical reference index absent"))?;
   639	        let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT k FROM i WHERE ti=?1 ORDER BY k")?;let mut rows=q.query([tx.num(&format!("{}.{index}",r.child))?])?;
   640	        let mut prior=None;let mut count=0u64;let mut visited=0u64;let mut meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);
   641	        while let Some(row)=rows.next()? {
   642	            if visited%256==0 {meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);}visited+=1;
   643	            let mark=meter.mark();let key=row.get_ref(0)?.as_blob().map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;meter.heap(key.len() as u64 * 64 + 8192)?;
   644	            let values=crate::key::decode(key).ok_or_else(||refusal("invalid historical reference key"))?;
   645	            if values.first().is_none_or(|v|*v==Value::Null) || r.table.is_expiring() && values.get(1).is_some_and(|v|matches!(v,Value::Int(at) if *at<=now)) {meter.release_heap(mark);continue;}
   646	            if prior.as_ref()==values.first(){count+=1;}else{prior=values.first().cloned();count=1;}
   647	            if count>u64::from(bound){return Err(refusal(format!("historical reference group {}.{} exceeds retirement bound {bound}; reconcile retained capacity in a bridge before adoption",r.child,r.column)));}
   648	            meter.release_heap(mark);
   649	        }
   650	    }}Ok(())
   651	}

//! Historical row transforms, separate from application-callable programs.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#2-language — the compiler and raw-schema boundary share a closed expression fragment.
use super::*;
use crate::ir::{Builtin, Expr, Program, ProgramKind, Stmt};
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Backfill {
    pub table: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub inputs: BTreeMap<String, ColumnType>,
    pub outputs: BTreeSet<String>,
    pub program: Program,
}
impl Backfill {
    pub fn key(&self) -> String { digest(&self.normalized()) }
    pub fn normalized(&self) -> Self {
        // Locations are diagnostic metadata, never transformation identity.
        let mut normalized = self.clone();
        fn expr(e: &mut Expr) {
            match e {
                Expr::Get(r) | Expr::Exists(r) => { r.site.clear(); for e in &mut r.key { expr(e); } }
                Expr::Scan(r) => {
                    r.site.clear();
                    for k in &mut r.prefix { if let crate::ir::IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(e)=k {expr(e);} }
                    if let Some(e)=&mut r.filter {expr(e);}
                    if let Some(r)=&mut r.range {for e in [&mut r.gt,&mut r.gte,&mut r.lt,&mut r.lte].into_iter().flatten(){expr(e);}}
                }
                Expr::Field { base, .. } | Expr::Unary { value: base, .. } => expr(base),
                Expr::Array(es) | Expr::Builtin { args: es, .. } => { for e in es { expr(e); } }
                Expr::Object(fs) => { for e in fs.values_mut() { expr(e); } }
                Expr::Shape { base, fields } => { expr(base); for e in fields.values_mut() { expr(e); } }
                Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => { expr(left); expr(right); }
                Expr::Block { body, value } => { statements(body); expr(value); }
                _ => (),
            }
        }
        fn statements(body: &mut [Stmt]) {
            for s in body {
                match s {
                    Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expr(value),
                    Stmt::Require { condition, site, .. } => { *site=None; expr(condition); },
                    Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => { expr(condition); statements(then_body); statements(else_body); }
                    _ => (),
                }
            }
        }
        statements(&mut normalized.program.body);
        normalized
    }
    pub fn admit(&self, schema: &Schema) -> Result<(), String> {
        let table = schema.table(&self.table).ok_or("backfill names an absent table")?;
        if table.ephemeral.is_some() || schema.maintain_for(&self.table).is_some() { return Err("backfill requires an authoritative durable table".into()); }
        if self.program.kind != ProgramKind::Query || self.program.native || self.outputs.is_empty() { return Err("backfill must produce a nonempty object patch".into()); }
        for c in &self.outputs {
            let kind = table.columns.get(c).ok_or_else(||format!("backfill output {}.{c} is not declared",self.table))?;
            if c=="id" || table.engine_field(c) || table.maintained.contains_key(c) || table.expire.as_ref()==Some(c) || table.not_before.as_ref()==Some(c) || !scalar(kind) {
                return Err(format!("backfill output {}.{c} must be an authoritative scalar, not an asset, lifetime or captured field",self.table));
            }
        }
        if !body(&self.program.body,schema) { return Err("backfill expressions allow deterministic scalar transforms, unique point lookups and bounded existence; no viewer, clock, ids, row collections, loops or writes".into()); }
        Ok(())
    }
}
fn scalar(k: &ColumnType) -> bool {
    match k {
        ColumnType::Optional(k) => scalar(k),
        ColumnType::Image { .. } | ColumnType::Video { .. } | ColumnType::File { .. } | ColumnType::List { .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { .. } => false,
        _ => true,
    }
}
fn body(stmts: &[Stmt], s: &Schema) -> bool {
    stmts.iter().all(|stmt| match stmt {
        Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expr(value,s),
        Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expr(condition,s),
        Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => expr(condition,s) && body(then_body,s) && body(else_body,s),
        _ => false,
    })
}
fn expr(e: &Expr, s: &Schema) -> bool {
    match e {
        Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Variable(_) => true,
        Expr::Input(_) | Expr::Merge(_) => false,
        Expr::Scan(r) => r.existence && r.after.is_none() && r.before.is_none() && r.prefix.iter().all(|k| k.scalar().is_some_and(|e|expr(e,s))) && r.filter.as_ref().is_none_or(|e|expr(e,s)) && r.range.as_ref().is_none_or(|r|[&r.gt,&r.gte,&r.lt,&r.lte].iter().all(|e|e.as_ref().is_none_or(|e|expr(e,s)))),
        Expr::Field { base, .. } | Expr::Unary { value: base, .. } => expr(base,s),
        Expr::Array(es) => es.iter().all(|e|expr(e,s)),
        Expr::Object(es) => es.values().all(|e|expr(e,s)),
        Expr::Shape { base, fields } => expr(base,s) && fields.values().all(|e|expr(e,s)),
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => expr(left,s) && expr(right,s),
        Expr::Block { body: stmts, value } => body(stmts,s) && expr(value,s),
        Expr::Get(r) | Expr::Exists(r) => s.table(&r.table).is_some_and(|t| t.index(&r.index).is_some_and(|i|i.unique && i.is_plain() && i.components.len()==r.key.len())) && r.key.iter().all(|e|expr(e,s)),
        Expr::Builtin { builtin, args } => matches!(builtin,
            Builtin::RequireUncapped | Builtin::ProjectFields { .. } | Builtin::OptionalField { .. } | Builtin::Len | Builtin::Count | Builtin::Slice | Builtin::Concat | Builtin::Lower | Builtin::Trim | Builtin::Text(_) | Builtin::Sha256 | Builtin::First | Builtin::Last | Builtin::At | Builtin::Min | Builtin::Max | Builtin::Divide { .. } | Builtin::Civil(_) | Builtin::Calendar { .. }
        ) && args.len()==builtin.arity() && args.iter().all(|e|expr(e,s)),
    }
}
//! Bind historical arguments without requiring them in the current row type.
use super::*;
use crate::{ast, diag::{type_error, unknown}, types::{Ty, scalar_to_column}};
use snapback4_core::schema::backfill::Backfill;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &mut Schema, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    let mut names = BTreeSet::new(); let mut outputs = BTreeSet::new();
    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d| &d.backfills) {
        if !names.insert((d.table.clone(),d.name.clone())) { diagnostics.push(unknown(&d.site,"duplicate backfill name on this table","give each historical transform its own name")); continue; }
        let decl = ast::ProgramDecl { delivery: None, held:false, predicts:false, server:false, kind:ast::ProgramKind::Query,
            name: format!("backfill{}{}",d.table,d.name), args:d.args.clone(), prelude:vec![],
            body:vec![ast::Stmt::Return { value:d.value.clone(), site:d.site.clone() }], native:false, site:d.site.clone() };
        let Some(bound) = program::Binder::bind(catalog,schema,"schema",&decl,diagnostics) else {continue};
        let Ty::Object(fields) = &bound.result else { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,"backfill returns an object patch","{ column: historicalValue }")); continue; };
        let Some(table) = schema.table(&d.table) else {diagnostics.push(unknown(&d.site,"backfill table is not declared","declare the candidate table"));continue};
        for (c,ty) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(c) {
                if !ty.assignable_to(&Ty::from_column(kind)) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("backfill {}.{c} returns {}, expected {}",d.table,ty.name(),kind),"return a value fitting the candidate column")); }
            }
            if !outputs.insert((d.table.clone(),c.clone())) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("backfills overlap on {}.{c}",d.table),"combine simultaneous outputs in one backfill")); }
        }
        let fill = Backfill { table:d.table.clone(), name:d.name.clone(), inputs:d.args.iter().map(|a|(a.name.clone(),scalar_to_column(&a.type_))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>(), outputs:fields.keys().cloned().collect(), program:bound.program };
        if let Err(why) = fill.admit(schema) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,why,"use deterministic historical fields and a scalar patch").guide("deploy")); }
        schema.backfills.push(fill.normalized());
    }
}

//! Candidate activation: project rows, validate invariants, build indexes
//! and derived rows, then publish the semantic generation in one commit.
// @ref LLP 3000.000#implementation-follow-up — one atomic activation boundary

pub(super) use super::activation_walk::walk;
use super::activation_walk::walk_metered;
use super::*;
use crate::ir::Program;
use crate::schema::{Constraint, materialize_optional_fields};
use crate::value::digest;
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

pub(super) type Identity = (Option<String>, Option<String>, Option<String>, Option<String>,
);

pub(super) fn identity(conn: &Connection) -> Result<Identity, RuntimeError> {
    let mut statement = conn.prepare_cached("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1")?;
    let mut get = |key| statement.query_row([key], |row| row.get(0)).optional();
    Ok((get("schema_hash")?, get("activation_hash")?, get("generation")?, get("device:generation")?,
    ))
}

impl Store {
    /// Install a device's schema. Server engines also supply their programs
    /// and derived-state builder through `activate` below.
    pub fn install(&mut self, schema: &Schema) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.activate(schema, None, false, |_, _| Ok(())).map(|_| ())
    }

    /// A device can discard an obsolete partition while atomically caching
    /// its replacement backend and reset watermark. It never migrates rows
    /// whose authority belongs to a previous server generation.
    pub fn install_device(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.install_device_checked(schema, metadata, reset, &BTreeMap::new())
    }

    pub fn install_device_checked(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool, expected: &BTreeMap<String, Option<String>>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.install_device_checked_with(schema, metadata, reset, expected, |_| Ok(()))
    }

    /// Device-owned sidecar changes share the schema installation transaction.
    pub fn install_device_checked_with(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool, expected: &BTreeMap<String, Option<String>>, prepare: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.install_device_checked_with_finish(schema, metadata, reset, expected, prepare, |_| {
            Ok(())
        })
    }

    /// Opening recovery and its final reads must finish before installation commits.
    pub fn install_device_checked_with_finish<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool, expected: &BTreeMap<String, Option<String>>, prepare: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    ) -> Result<R, RuntimeError> {
        let mut result = None;
        self.activate(schema, None, reset, |tx, _| {
            for (key, value) in expected {
                let held: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1", [key], |row| {
                        row.get(0)
                    }).optional()?;
                if &held != value { return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("the backend adoption capture is obsolete; retry sync".into())); }
            }
            prepare(tx)?;
            for (key, value) in metadata {
                // A device metadata namespace reset shares backend installation's transaction.
                if key.ends_with(':') {
                    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key >= ?1 AND key < ?2", params![key, format!("{key}\u{10ffff}")])?;
                } else { tx.set_meta(key, value)?; }
            }
            if reset { tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _device_base; DELETE FROM _device_snapshot;")?; }
            result = Some(finish(tx)?);
            Ok(())
        })?;
        Ok(result.expect("activation ran opening recovery"))
    }

    pub(crate) fn activate(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.activate_at(schema, programs, reset, crate::now_ms(), |tx, changed, _| build(tx, changed))
    }

    pub(crate) fn activate_at(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        // One-shot device preparation is never retried or given a larger cache.
        self.activate_transaction(schema, programs, reset, now, true, build)
    }

    pub(crate) fn activate_at_with_launch<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, check: impl Fn(&Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, mut build: impl FnMut(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl Fn(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    ) -> Result<(u64, R), RuntimeError> {
        let budget = if programs.is_some() { super::activation_cache::configured_mib()? } else { 0 };
        // The server builder owns only transaction-local work. Recreate all of
        // it on retry, with the same schema/programs/activation time.
        self.with_activation_cache(budget, |store| {
            store.activate_transaction_with(schema, programs, reset, now, allow_launch, &check, &mut build, &finish)
        })
    }

    pub(super) fn activate_transaction(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.activate_transaction_with(schema, programs, reset, now, allow_launch, |_| Ok(()), build, |_| Ok(())).map(|(generation, ())| generation)
    }

    fn activate_transaction_with<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, check: impl FnOnce(&Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    ) -> Result<(u64, R), RuntimeError> {
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — a removed opening boundary cannot lower durable source retirement time.
        let now=if self.server {now.max(self.meta("time_audience_clock")?.and_then(|s|s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(i64::MIN))}else{now};
        super::live_activation::configure(&self.conn,schema)?;
        let mut report = super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::default();
        let mut phase = std::time::Instant::now();
        if programs.is_some() {
            if let Some(estimate) = self.adoption_estimate(schema)?.filter(|e| e.seconds().1 > 1.0) {
                crate::diagnostic!("snapback4: atomic adoption: {}", estimate.summary());
            }
            let estimates = self.check_timeline_activation_at(schema, now, allow_launch)?;
            for estimate in estimates.iter().filter(|e| e.sources != 0) { crate::diagnostic!("snapback4: atomic feed adoption: {}", estimate.summary()); }
        }
        report.phase("read-only advice", &mut phase);

        // opening admission under the lock that owns the upgrades.

        if programs.is_some() { Self::initialize_change_evidence(&sql)?; }

        // an explicit device reset discards the old entries in this transaction.
        if before.0.is_some() && !reset && !keys_validated {
            let violation: Option<(String, i64)> = sql.query_row(
                "SELECT c.name, COUNT(*) FROM i JOIN _catalog c ON c.num = i.ti WHERE length(i.k) > ?1 GROUP BY i.ti ORDER BY i.ti LIMIT 1",
                [crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES as i64], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?))
            ).optional()?;
            if let Some((name, count)) = violation {
                let (table, index) = name.rsplit_once('.').unwrap_or((&name, "unknown"));
                return Err(crate::rows::index_key_refusal(table, index, count as u64));
            }
        }
        report.phase("key validation", &mut phase);
        let schema_hash = schema.hash();
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#6-devices-and-partitions — executable native content changes the existing semantic generation.
        let native: Option<String> = sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release_native_digest'", [], |r|r.get(0)).optional()?;

            let previous: Option<Schema> = previous.map(|s| {
                    serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::decode(e,RuntimeError::Store))
                }).transpose()?;

        }

                        old.defaults.get(column)==Some(default) && old.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind|!kind.is_optional())

                let mut counts = vec![0u64; fills.len()];

                    for (i,(column,_)) in fills.iter().enumerate() { if row.field(column).is_err() { counts[i] += 1; } }
                    Ok(())
                })?;
                for ((column,value),count) in fills.into_iter().zip(counts).filter(|(_,count)| *count > 0) {
                    let value = match value { Value::String(text) => format!("'{}'", text.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('\'', "\\'").replace('\n', "\\n").replace('\r', "\\r")), value => crate::wire::to_json(value).to_string() };
                    report.backfills.push(format!("{name}.{column} ← {value} on {count} {}",if count==1 {"row"} else {"rows"}));
                }
            }
        }
        let plans = super::activation_plan::plans(schema, if population_changed {None} else {previous.as_ref()});
        if let Some(previous)=&previous {
            for step in crate::successor::classify(previous,schema) {
                let parts=step.path.split('.').collect::<Vec<_>>();
                let label=if parts.first()==Some(&"tables") && parts.len()>=2 {
                    let name=parts[1];
                    let label=super::adoption_report::table_label(if schema.table(name).is_some(){schema}else{previous},name);
                    if label==name && parts.get(2)==Some(&"columns") && parts.get(3).is_some_and(|c|!c.starts_with('$')) {format!("{label}.{}",parts[3])}else{label}
                } else if let Some(position)=step.path.strip_prefix("maintains[").and_then(|s|s.strip_suffix(']')).and_then(|s|s.parse::<usize>().ok()) {
                    schema.maintains.get(position).map_or_else(||"view".into(),|m|m.label())
                } else {"backend".into()};
                let message=format!("{label} changed");
                if !report.changes.contains(&message){report.changes.push(message);}
            }
        }

        report.phase("preflight", &mut phase);
        if rebuilding {
            // @ref LLP 3000.000#implementation-follow-up — keys are still evaluated,
            // admitted and published in this transaction. Spill the key stream to
            // SQLite's temporary store, then insert in B-tree order instead of
            // repeatedly dirtying scattered main-database pages for every row.
            let ordered = programs.is_some() && plans.values().any(|plan| !plan.indexes.is_empty());
            #[cfg(test)]
            let ordered = ordered && !self.full_adoption;
            if ordered {
                super::activation_index::create(&tx)?;
            }
            // Removed names retain only their catalog number, never live row
            // images. Reintroducing a name starts empty, without resurrection.
            let active = super::activation_plan::active_names(schema);
            for (name, num) in &catalog {
                if !active.contains(name) {
                    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM r WHERE t = ?1", [num])?;
                    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti = ?1", [num])?;
                }
            }
            for (name, table) in schema.tables.iter().filter(|(_,t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
                let t = catalog[name];
                let plan = &plans[name];
                // Launch put() already built these fresh indexes under the
                // complete candidate schema. Keep the final invariant pass,
                // but do not delete and reconstruct the same entries again.
                if launches.iter().any(|launch| {
                    schema.timelines.iter().any(|timeline| {
                        timeline.name == launch.name && ["Posts", "Work", "Edges", "Feed"].iter()
                        .any(|suffix| timeline.table(suffix) == *name)
                    })
                }) { continue; }
                if !plan.project && plan.indexes.is_empty() { continue; }
                let started = std::time::Instant::now();
                for index in &plan.indexes { tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti = ?1", [catalog[&format!("{name}.{index}")]])?; }
                let work = crate::rows::adoption::Work::table(table);
                let scratch = |row: &Value| crate::rows::adoption::contribution_bytes(row, table);
                walk_metered(&tx, t, work, if work.index_entries != 0 { Some(&scratch) } else { None }, |id, mut row, contribution_meter| {
                    let keep = |column: &str| {
                            table.columns.contains_key(column) || (programs.is_none() && column == "pending")
                        };
                    let project = plan.project && row.as_object().is_ok_and(|fields| fields.keys().any(|column| !keep(column)));
                    // Persist only a destructive projection. Defaults and
                    // optionals are an interpretation of the stored image.
                    if project {
                        if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row { fields.retain(|column, _| keep(column)); }
                        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data = ?1 WHERE t = ?2 AND id = ?3", params![canonical_json(&row), t, id])?;
                    }
                    materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);
                    // A device's prediction mark is metadata, retained in
                    // storage but absent from the row the schema validates.
                    if programs.is_none() { if let Value::Row(fields) = &mut row { fields.remove("pending"); } }
                    // Maintained rows are replaced by the candidate builder.
                    if schema.maintain_for(name).is_none() { crate::engine::validate::validate_row(schema, name, &row)?; }
                    for index_name in &plan.indexes {
                        let index = &table.indexes[index_name];
                        let scratch = contribution_meter.mark();
                        let contribution = match index.components.first() { Some(crate::schema::IndexComponent::Contribution(m)) => Some(m), _ => None };
                        if let Some(m) = contribution {
                            contribution_meter.at(&format!("adopt {name}.{index_name}"));
                            contribution_meter.charge(crate::error::MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
                            contribution_meter.step(m.evaluation_steps())?;
                            crate::rows::adoption::charge(contribution_meter, crate::rows::adoption::contribution_bytes(&row, table))?;
                        }
                        for key in index_keys(index, &row, id) {
                            if key.len() > crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES {
                                return Err(crate::rows::index_key_refusal(name, index_name, 1));
                            }
                            if contribution.is_some() { contribution_meter.charge(crate::error::MeterKind::IndexEntries, 1)?; }
                            super::activation_index::insert(&tx, ordered, catalog[&format!("{name}.{index_name}")], &key, id)?;
                        }
                        contribution_meter.release_heap(scratch);
                    }
                    Ok(())
                })?;
                let label = super::adoption_report::table_label(schema, name);
                report.detail(format!("project/index {label} ({} indexes)", plan.indexes.len()), started);
            }
            if ordered {
                let started = std::time::Instant::now();
                tx.conn().execute_batch("INSERT INTO main.i(ti, k, id) SELECT ti, k, id FROM temp._adoption_index ORDER BY ti, k; DROP TABLE temp._adoption_index;")?;
                report.detail("ordered index insertion", started);
            }
        }
        report.phase("project and index", &mut phase);
        // Upgrade old stores and rebuild only when the schema changes. Counts
        // participate in the same activation transaction as rows and indexes.
        if cap_counts && (population_changed || tx.meta("cap_counts_schema")?.as_deref() != Some(&schema_hash)) {
            tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _cap_counts;")?;
            for (name, table) in schema.tables.iter().filter(|(_,t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
                if table.is_expiring() && table.constraints.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Cap { .. })) {
                    walk(&tx, catalog[name], |_, row| {
                        tx.update_cap_counts(name, table, None, Some(&row)).map(|_| ())
                    })?;
                }
            }
            tx.set_meta("cap_counts_schema", &schema_hash)?;
        }
        report.phase("cap counts", &mut phase);
        build(&tx, changed, &mut report)?;
        if rebuilding || reset || tx.meta(super::time_audience::PUBLICATION_STATE_KEY)?.as_deref() != Some("1") {
            tx.rebuild_time_audiences(schema, now)?;
        }
        report.phase("derived rebuild", &mut phase);
        if rebuilding && programs.is_some() && super::dormant::enabled(&tx)? {
            super::adopt_refusals::candidate(&tx,schema,if population_changed{None}else{previous.as_ref()},true)?;
        }
        if rebuilding {
            for (name, table) in schema.tables.iter().filter(|(_,t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
                let plan = &plans[name];
                // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — physical reuse cannot exempt rebuilt rows from unchanged invariants.
                let rebuilt = schema.maintain_for(name).is_some_and(|m| {
                    previous.as_ref().is_none_or(|old| crate::successor::maintain_rebuild_needed(old, schema, m))
                });
                if plan.project || rebuilt {
                walk(&tx, catalog[name], |_, mut row| {
                    materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);
                    if programs.is_none() { if let Value::Row(fields) = &mut row { fields.remove("pending"); } }
                    crate::engine::validate::validate_maintained_cache(schema, name, &row)?;
                    for (column, kind) in &table.columns { validate_assets(&tx, kind, row.field(column).ok())?; }
                    Ok(())
                })?;
                }
                for (index_name, index) in table.indexes.iter().filter(|(name, i)| {
                    i.unique && (plan.project || rebuilt || plan.indexes.contains(*name))
                }) {
                    validate_groups(&tx, name, index_name, index.components.len(), 1, true)?;
                }
                // @ref LLP 3000.039#2.2 — a `set null` cap skips its null group; adding or removing that exemption re-validates the cap.
                let exemption_changed = previous.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| old.tables.get(name).is_some_and(|old| old.constraints.iter().any(|c|
                    matches!(c, Constraint::Cap { by, .. } if old.cap_exempts_null(by) != table.cap_exempts_null(by)))));
                for constraint in table.constraints.iter().filter(|_| plan.project || rebuilt || exemption_changed) {
                    if let Constraint::Cap { by, max } = constraint {
                        let (index_name, _) = table.indexes.iter().find(|(_, i)| i.is_plain() && i.columns().starts_with(by)).ok_or_else(|| {
                                RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{name} cap has no matching index"))
                            })?;
                        validate_groups(&tx, name, index_name, by.len(), u64::from(*max), table.cap_exempts_null(by))?;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        report.phase("validation", &mut phase);
        if rebuilding {
            // A removed asset home is retired in the same transaction as its
            // projection. The ordinary collector removes bytes after commit.
            let homes: Vec<(String, String)> = tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT home_table, home_column FROM _assets WHERE home_table IS NOT NULL AND released_at IS NULL")?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
            for (table, column) in homes {
                if schema.table(&table).and_then(|t| t.columns.get(&column)).is_none_or(|kind| !contains_asset_type(kind)) && !super::dormant::holds_home(&tx,&table,&column)? {
                    tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _assets SET released_at = ?3 WHERE home_table = ?1 AND home_column = ?2 AND released_at IS NULL", params![table, column, crate::now_ms()])?;
                }
            }
        }
        let previous = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation'", [], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?.and_then(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
        let generation = if programs.is_some() && changed { previous.checked_add(1).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Generation("generation overflow".into()))? } else { previous.max(1) };
        tx.set_meta("schema_hash", &schema_hash)?;
        if let Some(hash) = &activation_hash {
            tx.set_meta("index_keys_validated", &format!("{}:{hash}", crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES))?;
        } else {
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key = 'index_keys_validated'", [])?;
        }
        if let (Some(hash), Some(programs)) = (&activation_hash, programs) {
            tx.set_meta("activation_hash", hash)?;
            tx.set_meta("generation", &generation.to_string())?;
            if changed {
                tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _generations(generation, schema, programs, at) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)", params![i64::try_from(generation).map_err(|_| RuntimeError::Generation("generation overflow".into()))?, canonical_json(schema), canonical_json(programs), crate::now_ms()])?;
            }
            // Inspection-only metadata shares the successful activation's
            // transaction, but never its schema bytes, hash or generation.
            if let Some(indexes) = &schema.authored_indexes.0 {
                let record = crate::schema::inspection::Record {
                    generation, schema_hash, indexes: indexes.clone(),
                };
                tx.set_meta(crate::schema::inspection::META_KEY, &canonical_json(&record))?;
            }
        }
        let after = identity(tx.conn())?;
        super::live_activation::finalize(&tx)?;
        let result = finish(&tx)?;
        super::live_activation::cleanup(&tx)?;
        report.phase("metadata", &mut phase);
        tx.commit()?;
        report.phase("commit", &mut phase);
        // Reopening an unchanged backend must not announce a new adoption.
        self.adoption_report = if changed || reset { report } else { Default::default() };
        // Historical ordinals stay in _catalog, but no runtime transaction can address memory tables.
        catalog.retain(|name,_| {
            !schema.tables.iter().any(|(table,t)| {
                t.ephemeral.is_some() && (name == table || name.starts_with(&format!("{table}.")))
            })
        });
        self.catalog = catalog;
        self.identity = after;
        self.accept_open();
        Ok((generation, result))
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — a key-bound proof must be invalidated even by direct SQL edits.
fn index_key_guards(conn: &Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let limit = crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES;
    let insert = format!("_index_keys_insert_{limit}");
    let update = format!("_index_keys_update_{limit}");
    let installed: i64 = conn.query_row(
        "SELECT count(*) FROM sqlite_schema WHERE type = 'trigger' AND name IN (?1, ?2)",
        params![insert, update], |r| r.get(0),
    )?;
    if installed != 2 {
        // A predecessor without both guards could have retained a proof across
        // physical edits. Install and revalidate in this activation transaction.
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key = 'index_keys_validated'", [])?;
        conn.execute_batch(&format!(
            "CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS {insert} AFTER INSERT ON i WHEN length(NEW.k) > {limit}
             BEGIN DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key = 'index_keys_validated'; END;
             CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS {update} AFTER UPDATE OF k ON i WHEN length(NEW.k) > {limit}
             BEGIN DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key = 'index_keys_validated'; END;"
        ))?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Equal prefixes are adjacent in an index. Validate uniqueness and caps
/// in one bounded pass per invariant, retaining one key rather than one
/// allocation for every group/row in a potentially large activation.
fn validate_groups(tx: &Tx<'_>, table: &str, index: &str, prefix_len: usize, max: u64, nulls_distinct: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    use crate::{error::MeterKind, meter::{Ceilings, Cut, Meter}, rows::word_heap as heap};
    // Only encoded group keys matter. Fetching source images here would
    // recreate an unmetered 500-row payload buffer after the metered rebuild.
    let mut statement = tx.conn().prepare_cached("SELECT k FROM i WHERE ti = ?1 ORDER BY k")?;
    let mut rows = statement.query([tx.num(&format!("{table}.{index}"))?])?;
    let mut previous: Option<Vec<Value>> = None;
    let mut count = 0;
    let mut visited = 0;
    let mut meter = Meter::new(Ceilings::job(), Cut::Abort);
    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {
        if visited % 256 == 0 {
            meter = Meter::new(Ceilings::job(), Cut::Abort);
            meter.at(&format!("adopt {table}.{index} groups"));
            meter.once(crate::meter::HeapSite::SqliteColumns, 2 * tx.page_size)?;
        }
        visited += 1;
        let scratch = meter.mark();
        meter.charge(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
        let key = row.get_ref(0)?.as_blob().map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        meter.heap(heap::key_decode(key.len(), key.len()))?;
        if let Some(values) = &previous {
            meter.heap(heap::slots::<Value>(values.len()))?;
            crate::engine::authorized_scan::InternalLedger::with_meter(&mut meter, |meter, scope| {
                for value in values { value.retained_internal(scope).charge(meter, scope)?; }
                Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(())
            })?;
        }
        let mut group = crate::key::decode(key).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Store("invalid rebuilt index key".into()))?;
        group.truncate(prefix_len);
        if !(nulls_distinct && group.contains(&Value::Null)) {
            if previous.as_ref() == Some(&group) { count += 1; } else { previous = Some(group); count = 1; }
            if count > max { return Err(RuntimeError::Constraint(format!("cannot activate {table}.{index}: existing group exceeds {}", if nulls_distinct { "a unique key".to_owned() } else { format!("cap {max}") }))); }
        }
        meter.release_heap(scratch);
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn contains_asset_type(kind: &crate::schema::ColumnType) -> bool {
    use crate::schema::ColumnType::*;
    match kind { Optional(inner) | List { of: inner, .. } | ListRange { of: inner, .. } => {
            contains_asset_type(inner)
        }
        Image { .. } | Video { .. } | File { .. } => true, _ => false }
}
pub(super) fn validate_assets(tx: &Tx<'_>, kind: &crate::schema::ColumnType, value: Option<&Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    use crate::assets::AssetKind;
    use crate::schema::ColumnType::*;
    match (kind, value) {
        (Optional(inner), Some(value)) => validate_assets(tx, inner, Some(value)),
        (List { of, .. } | ListRange { of, .. }, Some(Value::Array(values))) => { for value in values { validate_assets(tx, of, Some(value))?; } Ok(()) }
        (Image { .. } | Video { .. } | File { .. }, Some(value)) => {
            let (wanted, max, types) = kind.asset_leaf().expect("an asset leaf");
            if let Some(id) = crate::assets::asset_id(Some(value)) {
                if let Some(asset) = tx.asset(id)? {
                    if asset.kind != wanted { return Err(RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TYPE", message: format!("cannot activate: {id} is {}, not {}", asset.kind.word(), wanted.word()) }); }
                    if wanted == AssetKind::File && !crate::assets::file_accepts(types, &asset.content_type) { return Err(RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TYPE", message: format!("cannot activate: {id} is {}, which the column no longer accepts", asset.content_type) }); }
                    if asset.bytes > u64::from(max) { return Err(RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TOO_LARGE", message: format!("cannot activate: {id} has {} bytes; the column takes at most {max}", asset.bytes) }); }
                }
            }
            Ok(())
        }
        _ => Ok(())
    }
}

    63	fn references(kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value, out: &mut Vec<(String, String)>) {
    64	    match (kind, value) {
    65	        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) => references(inner, value, out),
    66	        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(values)) => {
    67	            for value in values {
    68	                references(of, value, out);
    69	            }
    70	        }
    71	        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => out.push((table.clone(), id.clone())),
    72	        _ => {}
    73	    }
    74	}
    75	
    76	fn rewrite_legacy_reference(
    77	    kind: &ColumnType,
    78	    value: &mut Value,
    79	    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
    80	) -> bool {
    81	    match (kind, value) {
    82	        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => {
    83	            let Some(next) = ids.get(table).and_then(|ids| ids.get(id)) else { return false };
    84	            *id = next.clone();
    85	            true
    86	        }
    87	        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) => rewrite_legacy_reference(inner, value, ids),
    88	        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(values)) =>
    89	            values.iter_mut().fold(false, |changed, value| rewrite_legacy_reference(of, value, ids) || changed),
    90	        _ => false,
    91	    }
    92	}
    93	
    94	fn rewrite_legacy_row(
    95	    table: &Table,
    96	    row: &mut Value,
    97	    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
    98	) -> bool {
    99	    let (Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields)) = row else { return false };
   100	    table.columns.iter().fold(false, |changed, (column, kind)| {
   101	        fields.get_mut(column).is_some_and(|value| rewrite_legacy_reference(kind, value, ids)) || changed
   102	    })
   103	}
   104	
   105	/// Rewrite references retained outside the active namespace alongside their
   106	/// active counterparts. References whose aggregate target was itself retired
   107	/// have no map and retain the ordinary dormant-lifetime refusal on restore.
   108	// @ref LLP 3000.007.003#3.0 — identity adoption covers every surviving typed reference, including dormant storage.
   109	pub(crate) fn rewrite_legacy_maintained_references(
   110	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   111	    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
   112	) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
   113	    if ids.values().all(BTreeMap::is_empty) || !enabled(tx)? { return Ok(0); }
   114	    let mut rewritten = 0_u64;
   115	
   116	    let cells = tx.conn().prepare(
   117	        "SELECT v.t,v.id,v.column_name,v.data,c.definition FROM _dormant_values v JOIN _catalog k ON k.num=v.t JOIN _dormant_columns c ON c.table_name=k.name AND c.column_name=v.column_name ORDER BY v.t,v.id,v.column_name"
   118	    )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?, row.get::<_,String>(4)?)))?
   119	        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
   120	    for (t, id, column, data, definition) in cells {
   121	        let definition: Column = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   122	        let mut value: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   123	        if rewrite_legacy_reference(&definition.kind, &mut value, ids) {
   124	            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_values SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3 AND column_name=?4", params![canonical_json(&value),t,id,column])?;
   125	            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
   126	        }
   127	    }
   128	
   129	    let rows = tx.conn().prepare(
   130	        "SELECT r.t,r.id,r.data,d.definition FROM _dormant_rows r JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=r.t JOIN _dormant_tables d ON d.table_name=c.name ORDER BY r.t,r.id"
   131	    )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?)))?
   132	        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
   133	    for (t, id, data, definition) in rows {
   134	        let table: Table = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   135	        let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   136	        if rewrite_legacy_row(&table, &mut row, ids) {
   137	            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_rows SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3", params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   138	            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
   139	        }
   140	    }
   141	
   142	    let narrowed = if super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? {
   143	        tx.conn().prepare(
   144	            "SELECT t,id,data,definition FROM _dormant_narrow_rows ORDER BY t,id"
   145	        )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?)))?
   146	            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?
   147	    } else { Vec::new() };
   148	    for (t, id, data, definition) in narrowed {
   149	        let table: Table = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   150	        let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   151	        if rewrite_legacy_row(&table, &mut row, ids) {
   152	            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_narrow_rows SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3", params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   153	            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
   154	        }
   155	    }
   156	
   157	    for (target, target_ids) in ids {
   158	        if target_ids.is_empty() { continue; }
   159	        let parent_t = tx.num(target)?;
   160	        for (old, next) in target_ids {
   161	            tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _dormant_refs(t,id,column_name,parent_t,parent_id,invalid) SELECT t,id,column_name,parent_t,?1,invalid FROM _dormant_refs WHERE parent_t=?2 AND parent_id=?3", params![next,parent_t,old])?;
   162	            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2", params![parent_t,old])?;
   163	        }
   164	    }
   165	    Ok(rewritten)
   166	}
   167	
   168	pub(super) fn capture_refs(
   169	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   170	    t: i64,
   171	    id: &str,
   172	    column: &str,
   173	    kind: &ColumnType,
   174	    value: &Value,
   175	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   176	    tx.conn().execute(
   177	        "DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
   178	        params![t, id, column],
   179	    )?;
   180	    capture_refs_preserving(tx,t,id,column,kind,value,&BTreeSet::new())
   181	}
   182	
   183	pub(super) fn capture_refs_preserving(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,column:&str,kind:&ColumnType,value:&Value,known:&BTreeSet<String>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   184	    let mut refs=Vec::new();references(kind,value,&mut refs);
   185	    for(parent,parent_id)in refs {if !known.contains(&parent_id){add_reference(tx,t,id,column,&parent,&parent_id)?;}}
   186	    Ok(())
   187	}
   188	
   189	fn add_reference(
   190	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   191	    t: i64,
   192	    id: &str,
   193	    column: &str,
   194	    parent: &str,
   195	    parent_id: &str,
   196	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   197	    let parent_t = tx.num(parent)?;
   198	    let invalid = !tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",params![parent_t,parent_id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?
   199	        && !tx.conn().query_row(
   200	            "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
   201	            params![parent_t, parent_id],
   202	            |r| r.get::<_, bool>(0),
   203	        )?;
   204	    tx.conn().execute(
   205	        "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _dormant_refs VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6)",
   206	        params![t, id, column, parent_t, parent_id, if invalid {2} else {0}],
   207	    )?;
   208	    Ok(())
   209	}
   210	pub(super) fn capture_through_parent(
   211	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   212	    name: &str,
   213	    table: &Table,
   214	    id: &str,
   215	    column: &str,
   216	    row: &Value,
   217	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   218	    if let Some(through) = &table.through {
   219	        if (through.fields.contains_key(column)
   220	            || matches!(
   221	                column,
   222	                crate::schema::through::PARENT_DEADLINE | crate::schema::through::DEADLINE
   223	            ))
   224	            && let Ok(Value::Id(parent_id)) = row.field(&through.reference)
   225	        {
   226	            add_reference(tx, tx.num(name)?, id, column, &through.parent, parent_id)?;
   227	        }
   228	    }
   229	    Ok(())
   230	}
   231	
   232	pub(super) fn save_cell(
   233	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   234	    name: &str,
   235	    table: &Table,
   236	    id: &str,
   237	    column: &str,
   238	    value: &Value,
   239	    capture: bool,
   240	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   241	    let t = tx.num(name)?;
   242	    // @ref LLP 3000.014.000#implementation-proof-clarifications — another row cannot overwrite a cell's live origin.
   243	    let live=table.engine_field(column) && table.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some());
   244	    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_values(t,id,column_name,data,live_source) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5) ON CONFLICT(t,id,column_name) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data,live_source=excluded.live_source",params![t,id,column,canonical_json(value),i64::from(live)])?;
   245	    if capture && !super::authority_origin::live_input(table,column) {
   246	        capture_refs(tx, t, id, column, &table.columns[column], value)
   247	    } else {
   248	        Ok(())
   249	    }
   250	}

   252	fn refuse(message: String) -> RuntimeError {
   253	    RuntimeError::Structural {
   254	        code: "E_ADOPT",
   255	        message,
   256	    }
   257	}

   259	pub(super) fn check_owner(tx: &Tx<'_>, name: &str, old: &Table, next: &Schema) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   260	    let Some(new) = next.table(name) else {
   261	        return Ok(());
   262	    };
   263	    let evidence = || -> Result<String, RuntimeError> {
   264	        let mut samples = super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
   265	        super::activation::walk(tx, tx.num(name)?, |id, _| {
   266	            samples.add(id, "storage ownership");
   267	            Ok(())
   268	        })?;
   269	        if samples.count == 0 {
   270	            let t = tx.num(name)?;
   271	            let count: i64 =
   272	                tx.conn()
   273	                    .query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1", [t], |r| {
   274	                        r.get(0)
   275	                    })?;
   276	            let ids = tx
   277	                .conn()
   278	                .prepare("SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY id LIMIT 5")?
   279	                .query_map([t], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))?
   280	                .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
   281	            return Ok(format!(
   282	                "{count} affected rows; sample ids [{}]",
   283	                ids.iter()
   284	                    .map(|i| super::adopt_refusals::bounded(i))
   285	                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
   286	                    .join(", ")
   287	            ));
   288	        }
   289	        Ok(samples.describe())
   290	    };
   291	    if new.ephemeral.is_some() {
   292	        return Err(refuse(format!(
   293	            "table {name}: {}; durable storage cannot become ephemeral; deploy without {name}, run `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`, then deploy the ephemeral table",
   294	            evidence()?
   295	        )));
   296	    }
   297	    if next.maintain_for(name).is_some() {
   298	        return Err(refuse(format!(
   299	            "table {name}: {}; authoritative storage cannot become a derived table; rename the derived table, or retire {name}, run `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`, then deploy",
   300	            evidence()?
   301	        )));
   302	    }
   303	    for column in old
   304	        .columns
   305	        .keys()
   306	        .filter(|column| authoritative(old, column))
   307	    {
   308	        if new.columns.contains_key(column) && !authoritative(new, column) {
   309	            return Err(refuse(format!(
   310	                "column {name}.{column}: {}; authoritative values cannot become maintained/captured values; rename the new column, or retire it, run `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`, then deploy",
   311	                evidence()?
   312	            )));
   313	        }
   314	    }
   315	    Ok(())
   316	}

   326	    report: &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport,
   327	) -> Result<BTreeSet<String>, RuntimeError> {
   328	    if !enabled(tx)? {
   329	        return Ok(BTreeSet::new());
