//! Release row lifetimes whose present values cannot inhabit the selected type.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#91-per-row-narrowed-value-dormancy — hide a reference-closed population; never coerce values.
use super::*;
use crate::schema::{ColumnType, Constraint};

pub(super) fn present(conn: &Connection) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(conn.query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_dormant_narrow_rows')",
        [],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?)
}

fn incompatible(tx:&Tx<'_>,schema: &Schema, name: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    incompatible_except(tx,schema,name,row,&[])
}
fn incompatible_except(tx:&Tx<'_>,schema: &Schema,name:&str,row:&Value,authority:&[String])->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    let table = &schema.tables[name];
    for (column,kind) in &table.columns {
        if table.engine_field(column) || table.maintained.contains_key(column) || authority.contains(column) {continue;}
        if let Ok(value)=row.field(column) {
            if incompatible_column(tx, table, name, column, kind, value)? { return Ok(true); }
        }
    }
    Ok(false)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — the warning and activation share the value predicate, including ledger-backed assets.
pub(super) fn incompatible_column(tx: &Tx<'_>, table: &Table, name: &str, column: &str, kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    if crate::engine::validate::validate_column(name, column, kind, Some(value)).is_err() { return Ok(true); }
    // @ref LLP 3000.038#1-the-type — asset facts belong to the ledger, including optional/list leaves.
    match super::activation::validate_assets(tx, kind, Some(value)) {
        Err(RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TYPE" | "E_ASSET_TOO_LARGE", .. }) => return Ok(true),
        Err(error) => return Err(error),
        Ok(()) => (),
    }
    Ok(table.constraints.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { field, min, format }
        if field == column && matches!(value, Value::String(s)
            if crate::engine::validate::validate_text(field, s, *min, *format).is_err()))))
}

fn refs(kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value, out: &mut Vec<(String, String)>) {
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), v) => refs(inner, v, out),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(vs)) => {
            for v in vs {
                refs(of, v, out);
            }
        }
        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => out.push((table.clone(), id.clone())),
        _ => {}
    }
}

pub(super) fn transition(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    next: &Schema,
    previous: Option<&Schema>,
    now: i64,
    restored_images: bool,
    source_definitions: &BTreeMap<String, Table>,
    authority_sources: &BTreeMap<String, Table>,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    // Backfill sources already retired through the ordinary action closure,
    // before patching. Never re-test predecessor deadlines on transformed rows.
    if next.backfills.is_empty() && !super::backfills::has_evidence(tx)? {retire_sources(tx,next,previous,now)?;}
    let have_dormant: bool = tx.conn().query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows)",
        [],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?;
    let type_change = previous.is_some_and(|old| {
        next.tables.iter().any(|(name, t)| {
            old.table(name).is_some_and(|p| {
                t.columns
                    .iter()
                    .any(|(c, k)| p.columns.get(c).is_some_and(|old| old != k))
                    || t.constraints
                        .iter()
                        .filter(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { .. }))
                        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                        != p.constraints
                            .iter()
                            .filter(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { .. }))
                            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            })
        })
    });
    if !have_dormant && !type_change && !restored_images {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let mut newly_retained = 0u64;
    // Stream on the primary key. Do not retain a store-sized list of images.
    restore_images(tx,next,now,source_definitions,authority_sources,false)?;
    tx.clear_activation_scan();
    // References to a hidden row hide their dependents, including memberships.
    loop {
        let mut removed = 0u64;
        for (name, table) in next
            .tables
            .iter()
            .filter(|(n, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none() && next.maintain_for(n).is_none())
        {
            let t = tx.num(name)?;
            super::activation::walk(tx, t, |id, mut row| {
                if let Some(source)=authority_sources.get(name) {
                    if crate::engine::through_activation::Retirement::retires_image(table,source,&row,now) {
                        tx.delete(name,source,&row)?;
                        return Ok(());
                    }
                }
                crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(
                    &mut row,
                    previous.and_then(|s| s.table(name)).unwrap_or(table),
                );
                let mut hidden = incompatible(tx,next, name, &row)?;
                for (column, kind) in &table.columns {
                    let mut edges = Vec::new();
                    if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
                        refs(kind, value, &mut edges);
                    }
                    for (parent, parent_id) in edges {
                        let missing = next.table(&parent).is_none()
                            || (!tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",params![tx.num(&parent)?,parent_id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?
                                && reserved(tx, tx.num(&parent)?, &parent_id)?);
                        if missing {
                            hidden = true;
                        }
                    }
                }
                if !hidden {
                    return Ok(());
                }
                let retained: bool = tx.conn().query_row(
                    "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
                    params![t, id],
                    |r| r.get(0),
                )?;
                let outputs:std::collections::BTreeSet<_>=next.backfills.iter().filter(|f|f.table==*name).flat_map(|f|f.outputs.iter().cloned()).collect();
                if retained && !outputs.is_empty() {
                    // Preserve the original source/provenance, updating only
                    // authoritative cells this release explicitly transformed.
                    let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;
                    let mut archived:Value=serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
                    if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut archived {for c in &outputs {if let Ok(v)=row.field(c){fields.insert(c.clone(),v.clone());}}}
                    tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_narrow_rows SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&archived),t,id])?;
                }
                for c in &outputs {
                    // Candidate-typed new references are absent from the old
                    // definition. Never erase an already-invalid binding.
                    let invalid:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND invalid=1)",params![t,id,c],|r|r.get(0))?;
                    if !invalid {if let Ok(value)=row.field(c) {super::dormant::capture_refs(tx,t,id,c,&table.columns[c],value)?;}}
                }
                if !retained {
                    newly_retained += 1;
                    let old = authority_sources.get(name).unwrap_or(table);
                    tx.conn().execute(
                        "INSERT INTO _dormant_narrow_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5)",
                        params![
                            t,
                            id,
                            canonical_json(&row),
                            canonical_json(old),
                            "type or dormant reference"
                        ],
                    )?;
                    for (column, kind) in &old.columns {
                        // Retained cells/whole rows already captured a lifetime; moving them must not erase deletion evidence.
                        let captured:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3)",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0))?;
                        if captured {
                            continue;
                        }
                        if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
                            if !super::authority_origin::live_input(old,column) {super::dormant::capture_refs(tx, t, id, column, kind, value)?;}
                        }
                        super::dormant::capture_through_parent(tx, name, old, id, column, &row)?;
                        // Nominal keep references may already be dangling. They have no bound lifetime.
                    }
                }
                tx.conn()
                    .execute("DELETE FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2", params![t, id])?;
                removed += 1;
                Ok(())
            })?;
            tx.clear_activation_scan();
        }
        if removed == 0 {
            break;
        }
    }
    // Consume only rows that survived the closed population, not hidden dependents.
    let restored:u64=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id)",[],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64))?;
    if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'narrowed',n.t,'',n.id FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id)",[])?;
    } else {
    let restored_rows=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT c.name,n.id FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=n.t WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id)")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,String>(0)?,r.get::<_,String>(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
    let mut restored_tables:std::collections::BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>>=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    for (name,id) in restored_rows {restored_tables.entry(name).or_default().push(id);}
    for (name, ids) in restored_tables {
        tx.set_meta(&format!("activation_narrowed_restored:{name}"),&ids.len().to_string())?;
        tx.set_meta(&format!("activation_narrowed_restored_ids:{name}"),&serde_json::to_string(&ids).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?)?;
    }
    }
    tx.set_meta("activation_narrowed_retained", &newly_retained.to_string())?;
    tx.set_meta("activation_narrowed_restored", &restored.to_string())?;
    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_dormant_narrow_rows.t AND r.id=_dormant_narrow_rows.id)",[])?;
    if newly_retained > 0 || restored > 0 {
        tx.set_meta("activation_population", "true")?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn reserved(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    if !present(tx.conn())? {
        return Ok(false);
    }
    Ok(tx.conn().query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
        params![t, id],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?)
}

pub(super) fn check_write(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    name: &str,
    _table: &Table,
    row: &Value,
    old: Option<&Value>,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if !present(tx.conn())? {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let id = row.field("id")?.as_str()?;
    let conflict = old.is_none() && reserved(tx, tx.num(name)?, id)?;
    if conflict {
        return Err(RuntimeError::Rule {
            table: name.into(),
            effect: if old.is_some() {
                crate::schema::Effect::Update
            } else {
                crate::schema::Effect::Insert
            },
        });
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn retire_sources(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,previous:Option<&Schema>,now:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    // An expired predecessor must not be archived and later revived without its lifetime.
    if let Some(previous) = previous {
        let retirement = crate::engine::through_activation::Retirement::new(next, previous, now);
        for (name, old) in &previous.tables {
            if next.tables.contains_key(name) && retirement.affects(name) {
                super::activation::walk(tx, tx.num(name)?, |_, row| {
                    if retirement.retires(name, &row) {
                        tx.delete(name, old, &row)?;
                    }
                    Ok(())
                })?;
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

// Restore retained source images before backfill evaluation. Candidate-owned
// output cells are checked after transformation, so a same-column enum rename
// does not preclude its own source image from entering the snapshot.
pub(super) fn restore_images(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,now:i64,source_definitions:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,authority_sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,for_backfills:bool)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    let rewritten=if for_backfills {super::backfills::columns(tx,next)?}else{Default::default()};
    let mut cursor = (0i64, String::new());
    loop {
        let item: Option<(i64,String,i64,i64)> = tx.conn().query_row(
            "SELECT t,id,octet_length(data),octet_length(definition) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE (t,id)>(?1,?2) AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_dormant_narrow_rows.t AND r.id=_dormant_narrow_rows.id) ORDER BY t,id LIMIT 1",
            params![cursor.0,cursor.1],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?,r.get(2)?,r.get(3)?))).optional()?;
        let Some((t,id,data_bytes,definition_bytes))=item else {break};
        // Retain one admitted provenance/image unit through decode, overlays,
        // reconciliation and encoding. Never own its blobs before this bound.
        let mut admission=crate::meter::Meter::new(crate::meter::Ceilings::job(),crate::meter::Cut::Abort);
        if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
            admission.heap((data_bytes as u64).saturating_add(definition_bytes as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
        }
        let (data,definition):(String,String)=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data,definition FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?;
        cursor = (t, id.clone());
        let name: String =
            tx.conn()
                .query_row("SELECT name FROM _catalog WHERE num=?1", [t], |r| r.get(0))?;
        let Some(table) = next
            .table(&name)
            .filter(|t| t.ephemeral.is_none() && next.maintain_for(&name).is_none())
        else {
            continue;
        };
        let old: Table =
            serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        super::dormant::check_owner(tx, &name, &old, next)?;
        let mut row: Value =
            serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        super::backfills::record_omissions(tx,t,&id,&row)?;
        crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,&old);
        if for_backfills && old.expired(&row,now) {
            // Expose an expired source only inside the uncommitted lifecycle
            // phase so the ordinary engine can close its reference actions.
            tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO r(t,id,data) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![t,id,canonical_json(&row)])?;
            continue;
        }
        let mut authority=crate::engine::through_activation::manual_authority_fields(next,&name,&old);
        if for_backfills { authority.extend(rewritten.get(&name).into_iter().flatten().cloned()); }
        if incompatible_except(tx,next,&name,&row,&authority)? {
            continue;
        }
        // Retained source deadlines precede projection, including removal of read-through itself.
        if crate::engine::through_activation::Retirement::retires_image(table,&old,&row,now)  {
            tx.delete(&name,&old,&row)?;
            continue;
        }
        // Actual deletion/reuse never recaptures a different parent lifetime.
        let invalid_columns = tx
            .conn()
            .prepare_cached(
                "SELECT column_name FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND invalid=1",
            )?
            .query_map(params![t, id], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))?
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
        let mut invalid=false;
        for c in &invalid_columns {
            if for_backfills && next.backfills.iter().any(|f| f.table==name && f.inputs.contains_key(c)) {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Structural{code:"E_ADOPT",message:format!("backfill historical reference {name}.{c} on {id} lost its parent lifetime")});
            }
            if table.columns.contains_key(c) && !super::authority_origin::obsolete_ref(tx,t,&id,c,table,Some(&old))? {invalid=true;}
        }
        if invalid {
            continue;
        }
        if super::authority_origin::live_row(tx,t,&id,&old,table)? {
            admission.heap(crate::engine::through_activation::restored_parent_scratch(next,source_definitions,&name,&old).saturating_add(crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table).saturating_mul(4)))?;
        }
        super::authority_origin::record(tx,t,&id,&old)?;
        super::authority_origin::validate(tx,next,authority_sources,&name,&old,&row,now,true)?;
        crate::engine::through_activation::forward_image(tx,next,authority_sources,&name,&old,&row,now)?;
        if old.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some()) {tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored(kind,t,col,id) VALUES('live-authority',?1,'',?2)",params![t,id])?;}
        for column in old.columns.keys() {
            if !table.columns.contains_key(column) {
                super::dormant::definition(tx, &name, &old, column)?;
                if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
                    super::dormant::save_cell(tx, &name, &old, &id, column, value, false)?;
                }
            }
        }
        if !for_backfills { if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row {
            fields.retain(|c, _| table.columns.contains_key(c));
        }}
        let values=super::dormant::restored_values(tx,t,&id,table,&mut admission)?;
        super::authority_origin::overlay(tx,t,&id,Some(&old),table,&mut row,values)?;
        if old.through.as_ref().is_none_or(|e|e.live.is_some()) {
            for column in table.columns.keys() {super::dormant::mark_live_column(tx,t,&id,column)?;}
        }
        crate::engine::through_activation::manual_image(tx,next,&name,&old,&mut row,now)?;
        if if for_backfills {incompatible_except(tx,next,&name,&row,&authority)?}else{incompatible(tx,next,&name,&row)?} {continue;}
        if !for_backfills {crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);}
        // Missing required new fields are a genuine backfill refusal, not dormancy.
        tx.conn().execute(
            "INSERT INTO r(t,id,data) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",
            params![t, id, canonical_json(&row)],
        )?;
    }
    tx.clear_activation_scan();
    Ok(())
}

   581	    // predecessor child. Capture the surviving post-cascade/set-null image.
   582	    if backfilling {if let Some(previous)=previous {for (name,old) in &previous.tables {
   583	        if next.tables.contains_key(name) || old.ephemeral.is_some() || previous.maintain_for(name).is_some(){continue;}
   584	        let t=tx.num(name)?;
   585	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1",[t])?;
   586	        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,mut row|{
   587	            materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,old);
   588	            tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![t,id,canonical_json(&row)])?;
   589	            for (column,kind) in &old.columns {if let Ok(value)=row.field(column) {
   590	                if !super::authority_origin::live_input(old,column) {capture_refs(tx,t,id,column,kind,value)?;}
   591	                capture_through_parent(tx,name,old,id,column,&row)?;
   592	            }}Ok(())
   593	        })?;
   594	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM r WHERE t=?1",[t])?;
   595	        for index in old.indexes.keys(){tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[tx.num(&format!("{name}.{index}"))?])?;}
   596	    }}}
   597	    super::backfills::transition(tx,next,previous,&source_definitions,now,report)?;
   598	    super::narrowed::transition(tx,next,previous,now,restored_images,&source_definitions,&authority_sources)?;
   599	    crate::engine::through_activation::reconcile_manual(tx,next,&authority_sources,now)?;
   600	    check_references(tx, next,&authority_sources)?;
   601	    // Restored rows have no predecessor indexes. Install the identity scan
   602	    // before the ordinary read-through barrier; preserve historical captures.
   603	    let identity_tables:BTreeSet<String>=if tx.meta("activation_population")?.as_deref()==Some("true") {
   604	        next.tables.iter().filter(|(n,t)|t.ephemeral.is_none() && next.maintain_for(n).is_none()).map(|(n,_)|n.clone()).collect()
   605	    } else {restoring.iter().map(|(n,_)|n.clone()).collect()};
   606	    for name in &identity_tables {
   607	        let table = &next.tables[name];
   608	        let t = tx.num(name)?;
   609	        let index = tx.num(&format!("{name}.byId"))?;
   610	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[index])?;
   611	        super::activation::walk(tx, t, |id, row| {
   612	            for key in index_keys(&table.indexes["byId"], &row, id) {
   613	                tx.conn().execute(
   614	                    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO i(ti,k,id) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",
   615	                    params![index, key, id],
   616	                )?;
   617	            }
   618	            Ok(())
   619	        })?;
   620	    }
   621	    for (name, table) in next.tables.iter().filter(|(_, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
   622	        let t = tx.num(name)?;
   623	        for column in table.columns.keys() {
   624	            if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
   625	                tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'column',v.t,v.column_name,v.id FROM _dormant_values v WHERE v.t=?1 AND v.column_name=?2 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id) AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=v.t AND n.id=v.id)",params![t,column])?;
   626	            } else {
   627	                let ids=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT v.id FROM _dormant_values v WHERE v.t=?1 AND v.column_name=?2 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id) AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=v.t AND n.id=v.id)")?.query_map(params![t,column],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
   628	                if !ids.is_empty() {tx.set_meta(&format!("activation_column_restored_ids:{name}.{column}"),&serde_json::to_string(&ids).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?)?;}
   629	            }
   630	            tx.conn().execute(
   631	                "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=_dormant_values.t AND n.id=_dormant_values.id)",
   632	                params![t, column],
   633	            )?;
   634	            tx.conn().execute(
   635	                "DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=_dormant_refs.t AND n.id=_dormant_refs.id)",
   636	                params![t, column],
   637	            )?;
   638	            tx.conn().execute(
   639	                "DELETE FROM _dormant_columns WHERE table_name=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?3 AND column_name=?2)",
   640	                params![name, column,t],
   641	            )?;
   642	        }
   643	        for index in table.indexes.keys() {
   644	            tx.conn().execute(
   645	                "DELETE FROM _dormant_indexes WHERE table_name=?1 AND index_name=?2",
   646	                params![name, index],
   647	            )?;
   648	        }
   649	    }
   650	    Ok(retained_through)
   651	}
   652	
   653	fn check_references(tx: &Tx<'_>, next: &Schema,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   654	    for (name, table) in next.tables.iter().filter(|(_, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
   655	        let t = tx.num(name)?;
   656	        let mut bad = super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
   657	        let mut statement=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT d.id,d.column_name,d.parent_id,d.invalid FROM _dormant_refs d LEFT JOIN r p ON p.t=d.parent_t AND p.id=d.parent_id WHERE d.t=?1 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r child WHERE child.t=d.t AND child.id=d.id) AND (d.invalid=1 OR (p.id IS NULL AND d.invalid!=2)) ORDER BY d.id,d.column_name")?;
   658	        let mut rows = statement.query([t])?;
   659	        while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {
   660	            let column: String = row.get(1)?;
   661	            let id:String=row.get(0)?;
   662	            if table.columns.contains_key(&column) && !super::authority_origin::obsolete_ref(tx,t,&id,&column,table,sources.get(name))? {
   663	                bad.add(
   664	                    &row.get::<_, String>(0)?,
   665	                    &format!(
   666	                        "{column} → parent {} (deleted/reused/missing)",
   667	                        row.get::<_, String>(2)?
   668	                    ),
   669	                );
   670	            }
   671	        }
   672	        if bad.count > 0 {
   673	            return Err(refuse(format!(
   674	                "table {name}: {}; dormant references cannot acquire another parent lifetime; keep the current release and repair the named references, or prune the dormant storage before restoring",
   675	                bad.describe()
   676	            )));
   677	        }
   678	        if let Some(through) = &table.through {
   679	            {
   680	                let mut bad = super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
   681	                super::activation_walk::walk_metered(tx,t,crate::rows::adoption::Work::DECODE,None,|id,row,meter| {
   682	                    let restored: bool = tx.conn().query_row(
   683	                        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
   684	                        params![t, id],
   685	                        |r| r.get(0),
   686	                    )?;
   687	                    if !restored {
   688	                        return Ok(());
   689	                    }
   690	                    // The captured deadline belongs to one parent lifetime,
   691	                    // even if an older schema allowed retargeting the active ref.
   692	                    let parent_t = tx.num(&through.parent)?;
   693	                    let current = row
   694	                        .field(&through.reference)
   695	                        .ok()
   696	                        .and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok());
   697	                    let mut evidence=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT parent_t,parent_id,(SELECT name FROM _catalog WHERE num=parent_t) FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3")?;
   698	                    for edge in evidence.query_map(
   699	                        params![t, id, crate::schema::through::PARENT_DEADLINE],
   700	                        |r| {
   701	                            Ok((
   702	                                r.get::<_, i64>(0)?,
   703	                                r.get::<_, String>(1)?,
   704	                                r.get::<_, String>(2)?,
   705	                            ))
   706	                        },
   707	                    )? {
   708	                        let (old_table, old_id, old_name) = edge?;
   709	                        if old_table != parent_t || current != Some(old_id.as_str()) {
   710	                            bad.add(
   711	                                id,
   712	                                &format!(
   713	                                    "{}.{}: recorded {} [{}], current {} [{}]",
   714	                                    name,
   715	                                    through.reference,
   716	                                    old_name,
   717	                                    old_id,
   718	                                    through.parent,
   719	                                    current.unwrap_or("<missing>")
   720	                                ),
   721	                            );
   722	                            return Ok(());
   723	                        }
   724	                    }
   725	                    if let Ok(Value::Id(parent_id)) = row.field(&through.reference) {
   726	                        if through.live.is_some() || crate::rows::Rows::restored_live_authority(tx,name,id)? {
   727	                            let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![parent_t,parent_id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   728	                            meter.heap((bytes.unwrap_or(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(4096))?;
   729	                        }
   730	                        if let Some(parent) = tx.get(&through.parent, parent_id)? {
   731	                            let parent =
   732	                                crate::schema::row_image(&parent, &next.tables[&through.parent]);
   733	                            // @ref LLP 3000.014.000#71-dormant-restoration-identities-are-a-relation — live policy restamps current inputs after preserving parent lifetime.
   734	                            if through.live.is_none() && !crate::rows::Rows::restored_live_authority(tx,name,id)? && through.fields.iter().any(|(capture, field)| {
   735	                                row.field(capture)
   736	                                    .ok()
   737	                                    .is_some_and(|value| Some(value) != parent.field(field).ok())
   738	                            }) {
   739	                                bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} parent {parent_id}: captured audience/identity changed",name,through.reference));
   740	                            }
   741	                        }
   742	                    }
   743	                    Ok(())
   744	                })?;
   745	                if bad.count > 0 {
   746	                    return Err(refuse(format!(
   747	                        "table {name}: {}; restore cannot rebind historical audience/deadline captures; restore the original parent reference and audience under the current release, then `snapback4 rollback <dir> --to N`, or explicitly `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`",
   748	                        bad.describe()
   749	                    )));
   750	                }
   751	            }
   752	        }
   753	    }
   754	    Ok(())
   755	}
   756	
   757	pub(super) fn delete(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str, name: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   758	    super::backfills::delete(tx,t,id)?;
   759	    if !enabled(tx)? {
   760	        return Ok(());
   761	    }
   762	    tx.conn().execute(
   763	        "UPDATE _dormant_refs SET invalid=1 WHERE parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2 AND invalid=0",
   764	        params![t, id],
   765	    )?;
   766	    let narrowed=super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())?;
   767	    let home=if narrowed {" OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?4 AND id=?2)"} else {""};
   768	    tx.conn().execute(&format!("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?3) WHERE home_table=?1 AND home_id=?2 AND (home_column IN (SELECT column_name FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?4 AND id=?2){home})"),params![name,id,crate::now_ms(),t])?;
   769	    tx.conn().execute(
   770	        "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
   771	        params![t, id],
   772	    )?;
   773	    tx.conn().execute(
   774	        "DELETE FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
   775	        params![t, id],
   776	    )?;
   777	    if narrowed {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;}
   778	    tx.conn().execute(
   779	        "DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
   780	        params![t, id],
   781	    )?;
   782	    Ok(())
   783	}
   784	
   785	pub(super) fn holds_home(tx: &Tx<'_>, table: &str, column: &str) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   786	    if !enabled(tx)? {
   787	        return Ok(false);
   788	    }
   789	    let narrowed=if super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? {" OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON n.t=c.num WHERE c.name=?1)"}else{""};
   790	    Ok(tx.conn().query_row(&format!("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_tables WHERE table_name=?1) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_columns WHERE table_name=?1 AND column_name=?2){narrowed}"),params![table,column],|r|r.get(0))?)
   791	}
   792	
   793	/// Fetch only destination cells on live transitions, after paying their full
   794	/// serialized/decoded coexistence in the owning activation page/row account.
   795	pub(super) fn restored_values(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,table:&Table,meter:&mut crate::meter::Meter)->Result<Vec<(String,String)>,RuntimeError> {
   796	    if !super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
   797	        return Ok(tx.conn().prepare_cached("SELECT column_name,data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?.query_map(params![t,id],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?);
   798	    }
   799	    let mut values=Vec::new();
   800	    for column in table.columns.keys() {
   801	        let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   802	        if let Some(bytes)=bytes {
   803	            meter.heap((bytes as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(1024+column.len() as u64*8))?;
   804	            let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0))?;
   805	            values.push((column.clone(),data));
   806	        }
   807	    }
   808	    Ok(values)
   809	}
   810	
   811	/// Narrowed forward correspondence has already run when this is called.
   812	/// Record cell-derived live provenance before consuming any source evidence.
   813	pub(super) fn mark_live_column(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,column:&str)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
   814	    if super::live_activation::active(tx)? && super::authority_origin::mode(tx,t,id)? != Some(1) {
   815	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'live-authority',?1,'',?2 WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND live_source=1)",params![t,id,column])?;
   816	    }Ok(())
   817	}

codex
The implementation stages patches before applying them and keeps per-row receipts, private reference bindings, and cohort snapshots. I’m tracing how those interact during reversal and re-entry, especially when multiple declarations touch the same historical inputs.

//! Operator preflight runs the real activation on a private disk-backed snapshot.
// REL3 owns dormant-visibility counts here; SURF clause narration lives in release_narration.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#92-exact-preview-and-operator-vocabulary — no live pointer, generation or audit changes.
use super::*;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
struct Snapshot(PathBuf);
impl Drop for Snapshot {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
    }
}

pub(super) fn preview(
    store_path: &Path,
    root: PathBuf,
    release: u64,
    options: &Options,
    held_owner: Option<&crate::store_owner::Owner>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let bound = super::releases::bound_at(root.clone())?;
    let project = Project { root };
    let native = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project,&bound.schema)?;
    let id = snapback4_core::ids::mint(snapback4_core::now_ms(), &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default())
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let snapshot = Snapshot(std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-release-preview-{id}")));
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&snapshot.0).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let path = snapshot.0.join("store.sqlite");
    // Probe the existing filesystem lock, independently of any HTTP address
    // hint. Keep a stopped store excluded through preview; never create a lock
    // sidecar or mutate its address record just to inspect ownership.
    let (live,_stopped_guard)=if held_owner.is_some() {(false,None)} else if store_path.exists() {
        let database=store_path.canonicalize().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        match snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(&database,&snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_path(&database),false,0) {
            Ok(owner)=>match owner.try_lock() {
                Ok(())=>(false,Some(owner)),
                Err(std::fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock)=>(true,None),
                Err(std::fs::TryLockError::Error(e))=>return Err(format!("preview owner probe failed: {e}")),
            },
            Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound=>return Err("preview cannot establish exclusive store ownership: missing owner sidecar; restart serve once, stop it, then retry".into()),
            Err(e)=>return Err(format!("preview owner probe failed: {e}")),
        }
    }else{(false,None)};
    // SQLite VACUUM INTO takes a coherent read snapshot even with a live WAL writer.
    // Busy/disk errors refuse this preview; never fall back to copying database bytes.
    if store_path.exists() {
    let source = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(store_path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    source
        .conn()
        .execute("VACUUM INTO ?1", [path.to_string_lossy().as_ref()])
        .map_err(|e| {
            format!("snapshot preview failed (retry after store/disk becomes available): {e}")
        })?;
    drop(source);
    }
    let mut store = snapback4_core::Store::open(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let held = store
        .release_status(0, None, None)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — preview uses the owning host's authoritative time, without minting or advancing anything.
    let activation_now=if live {
        let status=crate::release_control::request(store_path.parent().ok_or("preview store has no deployment directory")?,crate::release_control::Command::Status{after:0,items_after:None,event:None})?;
        if status["revision"]!=held["revision"] {return Err("preview raced release activation; retry against the current store".into());}
        status["clock"]["now"].as_i64().ok_or("serving owner does not expose its activation clock; restart it with this CLI before preview")?
    }else{snapback4_core::now_ms()};
    let mut activation =
        snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation::new(release, held["release"].as_u64());
    activation.expected_revision = held["revision"].as_u64();
    activation.native_digest = native.installed.map(|n| n.digest);
    activation.operation = "preview".into();
    activation.restore_dormant = !options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction") || options.flags.contains_key("restore-dormant");
    let mut steps = Vec::new();
    let estimate = store
        .adoption_estimate(&bound.schema)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
        .map(|e| e.summary());
    let candidate_schema=bound.schema.clone();
    let candidate_programs:std::collections::BTreeMap<_,_>=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|(p.program.name.clone(),p.program.clone())).collect();
    let mut hazards=Vec::new();
    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    let prior:Option<(String,String)>=store.conn().query_row("SELECT schema,programs FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')",[],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?))).ok();
    let prior_schema=prior.as_ref().map(|(schema,_)|serde_json::from_str::<snapback4_core::Schema>(schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).transpose()?;
    if let Some((schema,programs))=prior {
        let prior:snapback4_core::Schema=serde_json::from_str(&schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        steps=snapback4_core::successor::classify(&prior,&candidate_schema);
        let programs:std::collections::BTreeMap<String,snapback4_core::ir::Program>=serde_json::from_str(&programs).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        let target_label = format!("release {release}");
        let compatibility = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &prior, &candidate_schema, held["release"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0), &target_label, options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction"))?;
        for line in compatibility {
            if let Some(warning) = line.strip_prefix("WARNING ") { hazards.push(warning.to_owned()); }
            else { notes.push(line.trim_start_matches("note: ").to_owned()); }
        }
        let rollback=!options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction");
        let (behavior_warnings, behavior_notes) = super::release_narration::behavior(store_path, &store, &prior, &candidate_schema, &programs, &candidate_programs, &project, held["release"].as_u64(), &target_label, rollback)?;
        hazards.extend(behavior_warnings); notes.extend(behavior_notes);
        for name in prior.tables.keys().filter(|name|!candidate_schema.tables.contains_key(*name) && prior.maintain_for(name).is_none() && prior.tables[*name].ephemeral.is_none()) {
            let count:u64=store.conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM r WHERE t=(SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1)",[name],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            if count>0 {hazards.push(format!("table {name}: {count} rows become dormant; {target_label} lacks this table's behavior; preserve it in a bridge or accept its absence"));}
        }
    }
    super::release_safeguards::capture(&store)?;
    let mut engine = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release_prepared_at(
        store,
        bound.schema,
        bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
        &activation,
        options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
        activation_now,
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut planned = engine
        .store_mut()
        .release_status(0, None, None)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    planned["items"] = serde_json::to_value(
        engine
            .store_mut()
            .dormant_inventory(None, usize::MAX)
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let items: Vec<snapback4_core::store::DormantItem> = serde_json::from_value(planned["items"].clone()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut effects = super::release_safeguards::warnings(engine.store(),prior_schema.as_ref(),&candidate_schema,&candidate_programs,&items,release,!options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction"))?;
    effects.append(&mut hazards); hazards=effects;
    for fill in &engine.store().adoption_report.backfills { notes.push(format!("backfill: {fill}; confirm these historical values before selecting release {release}")); }
    steps.retain(|step|!step.message.contains("engine capture"));
    for step in &mut steps {
        if step.change==snapback4_core::successor::Change::Narrowing {
            step.change=snapback4_core::successor::Change::Additive;
            step.message=format!("{}; active rows satisfy the candidate after dormant projection",step.message.split(';').next().unwrap_or(&step.message));
        }
        if let Some(position)=step.path.strip_prefix("maintains[").and_then(|s|s.strip_suffix(']')).and_then(|s|s.parse::<usize>().ok()) {
            let label=candidate_schema.maintains[position].label();
            let visits=engine.store().adoption_report.views.iter().find(|view|view.name==label).map(|v|v.rows).unwrap_or(0);
            step.message=format!("maintain {label}: rebuilt from {visits} source rows");
            step.change=snapback4_core::successor::Change::Additive;
        }
        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.").unwrap_or(&step.path).replace(".columns.",".");
        let count=planned["items"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().find(|i|i["path"].as_str()==Some(path.as_str())).and_then(|i|i["rows"].as_u64()).unwrap_or(0);
        let unit = if step.path.contains(".columns.") { "cells" } else if step.path.contains(".indexes.") { "entries" } else { "rows" };
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — per-path prose uses the same disjoint counts as the inventory totals.
        let retained = if count == 0 && unit == "cells" {
            format!("no standalone {unit} become dormant (cells of any dormant rows are counted with those rows)")
        } else if count == 0 {
            format!("no {unit} become dormant")
        } else {
            format!("its {unit} become dormant ({count} {unit}; restored on forward)")
        };
        step.message=step.message.replace("its rows are lost", &retained).replace("its values are lost", &retained);
    }
    let checklist = super::release_bridge::checklist(held["release"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0),release,&hazards);
    if let Some(path) = options.flags.get("bridge") {
        let current = held["release"].as_u64().ok_or("a bridge needs a current deployed release")?;
        super::release_bridge::write(Path::new(path),store_path.parent().ok_or("no deployment directory")?,current,release,&checklist)?;
        eprintln!("bridge starter written to {path}; edit its current source and follow ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md before activation");
    }
    if options.json {
        println!("{}",serde_json::to_string_pretty(&serde_json::json!({"preview":true,"checklist":checklist,"hazards":hazards,"notes":notes,"current":held,"plan":planned,"steps":steps,"estimate":estimate,"phases":engine.store().adoption_report.summary(),"backfills":engine.store().adoption_report.backfills})).unwrap());
    } else {
        println!("preview: release {release} would be current as generation {}; live release unchanged", engine.generation());
        super::release_summary::print(prior_schema.as_ref(), &candidate_schema, &candidate_programs, &hazards, &notes, &steps, &items);
        if !options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction") { println!("  checklist: prove the named refusal cases and affected lists/counts as real accounts before release {release}; `--bridge <new-dir>` saves current source plus this app's ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md"); }
        println!("  storage: {}",snapback4_core::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::dormant(&items));
        if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") {
            for hazard in hazards { println!("  detail: WARNING {hazard}"); }
            for note in notes { println!("  detail: note: {note}"); }
            for step in steps {println!("  {}: {}",step.path,step.message);}
            for item in &items { println!("  {}: {} {} dormant (restored on compatible forward); sample ids [{}]", item.key, item.rows, item.unit(), item.sample_ids.join(", ")); }
            println!("{}", engine.store().adoption_report.summary());
            if let Some(estimate) = estimate {
                println!("{estimate}");
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
//! Owner-mediated release publication in the running HTTP process.
use super::*;
use crate::release_control::Command;
#[cfg(unix)]
use crate::release_control::Listener;
use snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation;

#[cfg(unix)]
pub(super) fn start(
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
    dir: std::path::PathBuf,
) -> Result<std::thread::JoinHandle<()>, String> {
    let store = shared
        .owner
        .as_ref()
        .ok_or("release control requires a persisted owner")?
        .path()
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let listener = Listener::bind(&dir, store)?;
    crate::lifecycle::managed_cleanup();
    crate::lifecycle::background("release-control", move || {
        listener.run(|command| apply(&shared, &dir, command))
    })
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — only assignments and committed rebind follow the database commit.
pub(super) fn apply(
    shared: &Shared,
    dir: &std::path::Path,
    command: Command,
) -> Result<Json, String> {
    match command {
        Command::Status {
            after,
            items_after,
            event,
        } => {
            let mut engine=lock(&shared.engine);
            let mut status=engine.store_mut().release_status(after,items_after.as_deref(),event).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            status["clock"]=json!({"now":shared.now(),"offsetMs":lock(&shared.clock).offset()});
            Ok(status)
        },
        Command::Prune {
            confirm,
            expected_revision,
        } => {
            let mut result = lock(&shared.engine)
                .store_mut()
                .prune_dormant(confirm, Some(expected_revision))
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if let Some(items) = result["items"].as_array_mut() {
                items.truncate(16);
            }
            result["items_after"] = result["items"]
                .as_array()
                .and_then(|items| items.last())
                .map(|i| i["key"].clone())
                .unwrap_or(Json::Null);
            Ok(result)
        }
        Command::Activate {
            release,
            expected_release,
            expected_revision,
            digest,
            rollback,
            restore_dormant,
            allow_feed_adoption,
        } => {
            if release == 0 {
                return Err("release id must be positive".into());
            }
            let project = Project {
                root: dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string()),
            };
            if !std::fs::symlink_metadata(&project.root)
                .is_ok_and(|m| m.is_dir() && !m.file_type().is_symlink())
            {
                return Err(format!(
                    "release {release} is not an immutable release directory"
                ));
            }
            if crate::release_files::digest(&project.root)? != digest {
                return Err("staged release content changed; redeploy its exact source".into());
            }
            if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
                return Err(format!(
                    "release {release} has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release"
                ));
            }
            let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
                return Err(bound
                    .diagnostics
                    .iter()
                    .map(ToString::to_string)
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                    .join("\n"));
            }
            let prepared = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project, &bound.schema)?;
            crate::release_files::link_store(&project.root)?;
            if crate::release_files::digest(&project.root)? != digest {
                return Err("release artifacts changed during preparation; retry after inspecting immutable source".into());
            }
            crate::release_files::durable(&project.root)?;
            let mut pointer =
                crate::release_files::Pointer::prepare(dir, release, expected_release, rollback)?;
            let options = ReleaseActivation {
                release,
                expected_previous: expected_release,
                expected_revision: Some(expected_revision),
                restore_dormant,
                native_digest: prepared.installed.as_ref().map(|m| m.digest.clone()),
                operation: if rollback { "rollback" } else { "deploy" }.into(),
            };
            let _requests=shared.release_requests.write();
            let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
            lock(&shared.readers).begin_adoption(&bound);
            let generation = match engine.adopt_release_at(
                bound.schema.clone(),
                bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()),
                &options,
                allow_feed_adoption,
                shared.now(),
            ) {
                Ok(generation) => generation,
                Err(error) => {
                    lock(&shared.readers).finish_adoption();
                    return Err(error.to_string());
                }
            };
            #[cfg(test)]
            test_work(&shared.release_committed);
            engine.set_prepared_native(prepared.module);
            engine.set_presence(Some(shared.presence.clone()));
            shared.refresh_time_audience_clock(&engine, shared.now());
            lock(&shared.readers).replace(&engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
            {
                let mut active = lock(&shared.bound);
                let mut advertised = lock(&shared.generation);
                let mut native = lock(&shared.native);
                shared.latency.adopt(&bound);
                *active = bound;
                *advertised = generation;
                *native = prepared.installed;
                let mut root = lock(&shared.release_root);
                *root = Some(project.root);
                shared.worker_revision.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Release);
            }
            crate::lifecycle::generation(generation);
            shared.publish_engine(&engine);
            // Completion I/O can fail, but never abandons the new runtime.
            pointer.finish(generation)?;
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
                "snapback4 serve: release {release} activated without restart as generation {generation}"
            );
            Ok(
                json!({"release":release,"generation":generation,"revision":expected_revision+1,"hot":true,"adoption":engine.store().adoption_report.compact(),"details":engine.store().adoption_report.summary(),"backfills":engine.store().adoption_report.backfills}),
            )
        }
    }
}
//! Human preview groups facts about one affected table/operation once.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#15-wave-15-preview-and-rollback-day5 — full evidence remains available in JSON and verbose output.
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
use snapback4_core::{Schema, ir::Program, store::DormantItem, successor::Step};
struct Group { warnings: Vec<String>, notes: Vec<String>, priority: u8 }
impl Default for Group { fn default() -> Self {Self {warnings:Vec::new(),notes:Vec::new(),priority:2}} }
fn table_prefix(text: &str, tables: &BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<String> {
    tables.iter().filter(|name|text.strip_prefix(name.as_str()).is_some_and(|tail|tail.is_empty() || tail.starts_with(['.',':',' ']))).max_by_key(|name|name.len()).cloned()
}
fn subject(text: &str, tables: &BTreeSet<String>, programs: &BTreeMap<String,Program>) -> String {
    // Read only generated subject positions, never arbitrary words inside an
    // authored predicate/refusal/value. Unknown formats remain release facts.
    if let Some(table)=table_prefix(text,tables) {return table;}
    for marker in ["table ","dependent ","backfill: "] {
        if let Some(tail)=text.strip_prefix(marker) && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    }
    let prefix=text.split(';').next().unwrap_or(text);
    if text.starts_with("queries/counts ") && let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" read ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    if text.starts_with("release ") && let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" safety dependencies on ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    if text.starts_with("rollback users get release ") {
        if let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" quotas on ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
        if let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once("'s ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    }
    if text.starts_with("release ") {
        if let Some(token)=prefix.split_whitespace().last() && let Some((owner,effect))=token.rsplit_once('.') {
            if effect=="require" {return format!("operation {owner}");}
            if tables.contains(owner) && matches!(effect,"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete") {return owner.into();}
        }
        for name in programs.keys() {
            if prefix.ends_with(&format!(" operation {name}")) {return format!("operation {name}");}
        }
    }
    "release behavior".into()
}
fn brief(text: &str) -> String {
    // Semicolons also separate authored guards and multiple cap declarations;
    // retaining the full fact avoids dropping their contracts or caveats.
    text.to_owned()
}
pub(super) fn print(current: Option<&Schema>, target: &Schema, programs: &BTreeMap<String,Program>, warnings: &[String], notes: &[String], steps: &[Step], items: &[DormantItem]) {
    let tables: BTreeSet<_>=target.tables.keys().chain(current.into_iter().flat_map(|s|s.tables.keys())).cloned().collect();
    let mut groups=BTreeMap::<String,Group>::new();
    for (warning, lines) in [(true,warnings),(false,notes)] {
        for line in lines {
            let group=groups.entry(subject(line,&tables,programs)).or_default();
            let priority=if line.contains("safety guard") || line.contains(".require;") {0} else if line.contains("confidentiality") || line.contains("safety rule") {1} else {2};
            group.priority=group.priority.min(priority);
            let text=if priority==0 {line.clone()} else {brief(line)};
            let dest=if warning {&mut group.warnings} else {&mut group.notes};
            if !dest.contains(&text) {dest.push(text);}
        }
    }
    for step in steps {
        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.").unwrap_or(&step.path);
        let key=table_prefix(path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||path.to_owned());
        if path.strip_prefix(&key).is_some_and(|tail|tail.starts_with(".indexes.")) {continue;}
        let group=groups.entry(key).or_default();
        // Keep each user-visible source change even when another fact already
        // names this table. Only index plumbing is verbose-only.
        let detail=brief(&format!("{}: {}",path.replace(".columns.","."),step.message));
        if !group.notes.contains(&detail) {group.notes.push(detail);}
    }
    let mut storage=BTreeMap::<String,[u64;3]>::new();
    for item in items.iter().filter(|item|item.rows>0 && item.kind!="backfill") {
        let name=table_prefix(&item.path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||item.path.clone());
        let slot=match item.kind.as_str() {"column"=>1,"index"=>2,_=>0};
        storage.entry(name).or_default()[slot]+=item.rows;
    }
    for (name,[rows,cells,indexes]) in storage {
        groups.entry(name).or_default().notes.push(format!("retained: {rows} dormant rows, {cells} cells, {indexes} index entries"));
    }
    for item in items.iter().filter(|item|item.kind=="backfill" && item.rows>0) {
        let name=table_prefix(&item.path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||item.path.clone());
        groups.entry(name).or_default().notes.push(format!("retained: {} private backfill preimages ({} bytes)",item.rows,item.bytes));
    }
    let mut groups=groups.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    groups.sort_by_key(|(name,g)|(g.priority,name.clone()));
    for (name,mut group) in groups {
        let warning=!group.warnings.is_empty();
        group.warnings.append(&mut group.notes);
        println!("  {} {name}: {}{}",if warning {"WARNING"} else {"note:"},group.warnings.join("; "),if warning {"; verify these cases or preserve the current behavior in a bridge release"} else {""});
    }
}

#[test]
fn authored_values_cannot_assign_a_guard_to_an_unrelated_table() {
    let tables=BTreeSet::from(["unrelated".to_owned(),"workspace.plans".to_owned()]);
    let line="release 1 loosens book.require; old: require title != 'unrelated' else STOP; new: allow; rollback users lose a current safety guard";
    assert_eq!(subject(line,&tables,&BTreeMap::new()),"operation book");
    assert_eq!(subject("workspace.plans.state: 2 rows become dormant",&tables,&BTreeMap::new()),"workspace.plans");
}
