//! Release activation extends the existing engine transaction and generation.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — CAS includes every activation, not only semantic changes.
use super::*;
use rusqlite::OptionalExtension;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ReleaseActivation {
    pub release: u64,
    pub expected_previous: Option<u64>,
    pub expected_revision: Option<u64>,
    pub restore_dormant: bool,
    pub native_digest: Option<String>,
    pub operation: String,
}
impl ReleaseActivation {
    pub fn new(release: u64, expected_previous: Option<u64>) -> Self {
        Self {
            release,
            expected_previous,
            expected_revision: None,
            restore_dormant: true,
            native_digest: None,
            operation: "activate".into(),
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn check(&self, conn: &rusqlite::Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let actual: Option<String> = conn
            .query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release'", [], |r| {
                r.get(0)
            })
            .optional()?;
        if actual
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok() != self.expected_previous)
        {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("current release changed; run `snapback4 releases <dir>` and retry the selected release".into()));
        }
        let revision: Option<String> = conn
            .query_row(
                "SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release_revision'",
                [],
                |r| r.get(0),
            )
            .optional()?;
        let revision = revision.and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
        if self.expected_revision.is_some_and(|n| n != revision) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("activation revision changed (including rollback/equal-content activation); run `snapback4 releases <dir>` and retry".into()));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl Engine {
    pub fn new_release(
        store: Store,
        schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        release: u64,
        expected_previous: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new_release_with_feed_adoption(
            store,
            schema,
            programs,
            release,
            expected_previous,
            false,
        )
    }
    pub fn new_release_with_feed_adoption(
        store: Store,
        schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        release: u64,
        expected_previous: Option<u64>,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new_release_prepared(
            store,
            schema,
            programs,
            &ReleaseActivation::new(release, expected_previous),
            allow_feed_adoption,
        )
    }
    pub fn new_release_prepared(
        store: Store, schema: Schema, programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation, allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new_release_prepared_at(store,schema,programs,options,allow_feed_adoption,crate::now_ms())
    }
    pub fn new_release_prepared_at(
        mut store: Store,
        mut schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
        now: i64,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        prepare(&mut schema)?;
        let programs = programs.into_iter().map(|p| (p.name.clone(), p)).collect();
        let (generation, metadata) = Self::activate(
            &mut store,
            &schema,
            &programs,
            Some(options),
            now,
            allow_feed_adoption,
            None,
            None,
        )?;
        store.exclude_ephemeral(&schema);
        Ok(Self {
            timeline_schedule: timeline::Schedule::default(),
            ephemeral: std::sync::Arc::new(ephemeral::Hub::new(
                metadata.store_id,
                generation,
                &schema,
            )),
            store,
            schema,
            programs,
            generation,
            expiry_after: BTreeMap::new(),
            dev_effects: false,
            ceilings: Ceilings::default(),
            entropy: crate::ids::seed(),
            minter: crate::ids::Mint::default(),
            #[cfg(feature = "native")]
            native: None,
            #[cfg(feature = "native")]
            native_logs: native::Logs::default(),
            presence: None,
        })
    }
    /// No replacement engine, post-commit SQL read, or fallible ephemeral swap.
    pub fn adopt_release(
        &mut self, schema: Schema, programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation, allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    ) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.adopt_release_at(schema,programs,options,allow_feed_adoption,crate::now_ms())
    }
    pub fn adopt_release_at(
        &mut self,
        mut schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
        now: i64,
    ) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        prepare(&mut schema)?;
        let programs = programs.into_iter().map(|p| (p.name.clone(), p)).collect();
        drop(self.ephemeral.lock()?);
        let (generation, metadata) = Self::activate(
            &mut self.store,
            &schema,
            &programs,
            Some(options),
            now,
            allow_feed_adoption,
            None,
            None,
        )?;
        self.store.exclude_ephemeral(&schema);
        self.timeline_schedule = timeline::Schedule::default();
        self.schema = schema;
        self.programs = programs;
        self.generation = generation;
        self.expiry_after.clear();
        self.dev_effects = false;
        self.ephemeral
            .rebind_committed(metadata.store_id, generation, &self.schema);
        Ok(generation)
    }
    #[cfg(feature = "native")]
    pub fn set_prepared_native(&mut self, module: Option<std::sync::Arc<native::Module>>) {
        self.native = module;
    }
}

fn prepare(schema: &mut Schema) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    columns::validate_before_prepare(schema)?;
    schema.check_row_view_audiences()?;
    schema.prepare_contribution_indexes();
    schema.prepare_delivery_lookup();
    if let Some(issue) = crate::schema::validate(schema).first() {
        return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
            "{}: {}",
            issue.path, issue.message
        )));
    }
    Ok(())
}
//! Server activation builder; all fallible work precedes commit.
use super::*;
impl Engine {
    pub(super) fn activate(
        store: &mut Store,
        schema: &Schema,
        programs: &BTreeMap<String, Program>,
        release: Option<&release::ReleaseActivation>,
        now: i64,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
        expected_release: Option<u64>,
        expected_backend: Option<&crate::store::opening::Backend>,
    ) -> Result<(u64, crate::store::opening::Metadata), RuntimeError> {
        schema.validate_column_owners()?;
        for program in programs.values() {
            crate::schema::ephemeral::admit_program(schema, program)?;
            admission::check_state_predicates(schema, program)?;
        }
        for program in programs.values() {
            for table in crate::ir::read_sites(program)
                .into_iter()
                .map(|s| s.table)
                .chain(crate::ir::write_tables(program).into_iter().map(|(t, _)| t))
            {
                schema.refuse_private(&table)?;
            }
        }
        for schedule in &schema.schedules {
            if !schedule.valid()
            {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
                    "schedule {} has an invalid period or offset",
                    schedule.name
                )));
            }
            let Some(program) = programs
                .get(&schedule.target)
                .filter(|p| matches!(p.kind, ProgramKind::Job | ProgramKind::Action))
            else {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
                    "schedule {} names missing job or action {}; restore or remove the schedule",
                    schedule.name, schedule.target
                )));
            };
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action {
                let seat = schedule
                    .service
                    .as_deref()
                    .filter(|seat| crate::identity::is_service(seat))
                    .ok_or_else(|| {
                        RuntimeError::Schema(
                            "a scheduled action requires its declared service seat".into(),
                        )
                    })?;
                actions::authorize(program, seat)?;
            } else if schedule.service.is_some() {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(
                    "a scheduled job cannot name a service seat".into(),
                ));
            }
            if program.args.iter().any(|(name, kind)| {
                !matches!(name.as_str(), "periodStart" | "periodEnd")
                    || *kind != crate::ir::ArgType::Time
            }) {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(
                    "scheduled work takes periodStart: time and periodEnd: time, or nothing".into(),
                ));
            }
        }
        let prior_items = std::cell::RefCell::new(Vec::new());
        let population=std::cell::Cell::new(None);
        let outcome=store.activate_at_with_launch(schema, Some(programs), false, now, allow_feed_adoption, |conn| {
            Store::check_selected_release(conn, expected_release)?;
            if let Some(backend) = expected_backend { backend.check(conn)?; }
            if let Some(options)=release {
                options.check(conn)?;
                *prior_items.borrow_mut()=crate::store::release_history::inventory(conn,None,usize::MAX)?;
                crate::store::dormant::enable(conn)?;
                conn.execute("INSERT INTO _meta(key,value) VALUES('release_native_digest',?1) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value",[options.native_digest.as_deref().unwrap_or("")])?;
                conn.execute("INSERT INTO _meta(key,value) VALUES('activation_restore',?1) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value",[if options.restore_dormant {"true"}else{"false"}])?;
            }
            Ok(())
        }, |tx, changed, report| {
            let now=now.max(tx.time_audience_clock()?);
            if let Some(options)=release {tx.set_meta("release",&options.release.to_string())?;}
            use rusqlite::OptionalExtension;
            let repair_live_sums = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'live_sum_candidates'", [], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?.as_deref() != Some("1");
            let previous: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation = (SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation')", [], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
            let previous: Option<Schema> = previous.map(|text| serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::decode(e, RuntimeError::Store))).transpose()?;
            let needs_maintained_id_marker = (schema.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none())
                || previous.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| old.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none())))
                && tx.meta(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META)?.as_deref() != Some(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION);
            if !changed && !repair_live_sums && !needs_maintained_id_marker { return Ok(()); }
            let repair_maintained_ids = needs_maintained_id_marker
                && previous.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| old.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none()));
            if changed {
                let epoch = tx.next_seq()?;
                for table in partition::changed_group_audiences(previous.as_ref(), schema) {
                    tx.set_meta(&partition::audience_epoch_key(&table), &epoch.to_string())?;
                    tx.set_meta(&partition::audience_epoch_time_key(&table), &now.to_string())?;
                }
            }
            let population_changed=tx.meta("activation_population")?.as_deref()==Some("true");
            let rebuild: Vec<_> = schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| {
                (changed && (population_changed || previous.as_ref().is_none_or(|old| crate::successor::maintain_rebuild_needed(old, schema, m))))
                    || (repair_maintained_ids && m.row_key.is_none())
                    || (repair_live_sums && matches!(m.kind, crate::schema::MaintainKind::Sum { .. }) && schema.table(&m.source).is_some_and(|source| source.is_expiring()))
            }).collect();
            tx.set_meta("live_sum_candidates", "1")?;
            if needs_maintained_id_marker {
                // This write is inside the activation transaction: a failed
                // rebuild leaves both old rows and the old/missing marker. A
                // first aggregate has no legacy target identity to rewrite,
                // but records the current address function for later opens.
                tx.set_meta(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META, crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION)?;
            }
            let attribution = recorded::paths_changed(schema, previous.as_ref());
            let columns = column_activation::changed_parents(schema, if population_changed {None} else {previous.as_ref()});
            if rebuild.is_empty() && !attribution && columns.is_empty() && !repair_maintained_ids { return Ok(()); }
            let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
            let phase = std::time::Instant::now();
            if attribution {
                // This runner owns only activation rewrites; ordinary rebuilds
                // below retain their commit and partition accounting.
                let mut capture = Runner::new(tx, schema, Context::system().at(now), Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort), seq);
                capture.capture_activation(previous.as_ref(), Some(report))?;
                report.detail("capture recorded paths", phase);
                // A newly introduced aggregate has no legacy identity map to
                // preserve. Keep its event-free recorded-path rebuild out of
                // commit accounting, as activation did before the address
                // migration existed.
                if !repair_maintained_ids {
                    capture.rebuild_recorded_activation(previous.as_ref(), &rebuild.iter().copied().filter(|m| m.private.is_none()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), report)?;
                }
            }
            let mut runner = Runner::new(tx, schema, Context::system().at(now), Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort), seq);
            if repair_maintained_ids {
                let started = std::time::Instant::now();
                let rewritten = runner.rewrite_legacy_maintained_references(&rebuild.iter().copied().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>(), previous.as_ref(), Some(tx))?;
                if rewritten != 0 { report.changes.push(format!("rewrote {rewritten} legacy aggregate references")); }
                report.detail("rewrite legacy aggregate references", started);
            }
            // Legacy maps must be captured before any activation rebuild can
            // remove their target rows. Recorded values themselves are
            // stamped first so their historical paths may still traverse the
            // predecessor addresses.
            if attribution && repair_maintained_ids {
                runner.rebuild_recorded_activation(previous.as_ref(), &rebuild.iter().copied().filter(|m| m.private.is_none()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), report)?;
            }
            runner.activate_columns(previous.as_ref(), &rebuild, &columns, Some(report))?;
            for maintain in rebuild.into_iter().filter(|m| m.private.is_none()) {
                if !recorded::table_paths_changed(schema.table(&maintain.source), previous.as_ref().and_then(|s| s.table(&maintain.source))) {
                    runner.rebuild_maintain_reported(maintain, report)?;
                }
            }
            let events = runner.events.take();
            let tables: Vec<String> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
            let written = runner.rows_written;
            drop(runner);
            if written > 0 || !columns.is_empty() {
                tx.record_commit(seq, now, "rebuild", crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL, "rebuild", &tables, written)?;
                partition::append(tx, seq, &events)?;
            }
            Ok(())
        }, |tx| {
            if let Some(options)=release {
                crate::store::release_history::finalize_restorations(tx)?;
                let mut items=crate::store::release_history::inventory(tx.conn(),None,usize::MAX)?;
                let dormant_items=items.len();
                let mut restored_items=0usize;
                for item in &mut items {
                    let before=prior_items.borrow().iter().find(|i|i.key==item.key).cloned();
                    let count=before.as_ref().map_or(0,|i|i.rows);
                    item.before_rows=Some(count);item.after_rows=Some(item.rows);
                    item.before_sample_ids=Some(before.map_or_else(Vec::new,|i|i.sample_ids));
                    let restored=if item.kind=="rows" {
                        tx.meta(&format!("activation_narrowed_restored:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)
                    } else if item.kind=="column" {tx.meta(&format!("activation_column_restored:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)} else {0};
                    item.restored_rows=Some(restored);
                    if restored>0 {item.action="partially restored".into();restored_items+=1;}
                }
                let remaining:std::collections::BTreeSet<_>=items.iter().map(|i|i.key.clone()).collect();
                for mut item in prior_items.borrow().iter().filter(|i|!remaining.contains(&i.key)).cloned() {
                    let restored=if matches!(item.kind.as_str(),"rows"|"column") {
                        let prefix=if item.kind=="rows" {"activation_narrowed_restored"} else {"activation_column_restored"};
                        tx.meta(&format!("{prefix}:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)
                    } else if item.kind=="table" {
                        tx.meta(&format!("activation_table_restored:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)
                    } else {item.rows};
                    item.action=if restored>0 || item.definition_only {"restored"} else {"removed without restoration"}.into();
                    item.before_rows=Some(item.rows);item.after_rows=Some(0);item.restored_rows=Some(restored);item.before_sample_ids=Some(item.sample_ids.clone());
                    if restored>0 || item.definition_only {restored_items+=1;}
                    items.push(item);
                }
                population.set(Some((tx.meta("activation_narrowed_retained")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),tx.meta("activation_narrowed_restored")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0))));
                crate::store::release_history::event(tx,&options.operation,Some(options.release),options.expected_previous,serde_json::json!({"dormant_items":dormant_items,"restored_items":restored_items,"native_digest":options.native_digest,"narrowed_retained":tx.meta("activation_narrowed_retained")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),"narrowed_restored":tx.meta("activation_narrowed_restored")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)}),&items)?;
                tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key IN ('activation_restore','activation_population','activation_narrowed_retained','activation_narrowed_restored') OR key LIKE 'activation_narrowed_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_column_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_table_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_table_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_narrowed_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_column_restored_ids:%'",[])?;
            }
            crate::store::opening::Metadata::read(tx)
        });
        if outcome.is_ok() {
            if let Some((retained,restored))=population.get().filter(|(a,b)|*a>0 || *b>0) {
                store.adoption_report.changes.insert(0,format!("{retained} newly dormant {}; {restored} narrowed {} restored", if retained == 1 { "row" } else { "rows" }, if restored == 1 { "row" } else { "rows" }));
            }
        }
        if outcome.is_ok() {
            if let Ok(items)=store.dormant_inventory(None,usize::MAX) {
                if release.is_some() { store.adoption_report.changes.insert(0, crate::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::dormant(&items)); }
            }
        }
        outcome
    }
}
//! A development reset: the adopted schema over an empty store, under a new
//! store identity, optionally reseeded, in one SQLite transaction.
// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.25 — what "fresh" keeps and what it empties; why the head keeps counting.
use super::*;

/// Tables a reset keeps whole: the store's own metadata, the catalog that
/// numbers tables and indexes, and the adopted generations.
const KEPT: [&str; 3] = ["_meta", "_catalog", "_generations"];

/// Identity tables a reset empties except for service seats: the dev
/// worker's session and `snapback4 token` seats outlive a reset, accounts,
/// guests and their sessions do not.
const SERVICE_SEATS: [(&str, &str); 5] = [
    ("_principals", "id"), ("_sessions", "principal"), ("_read_sessions", "principal"),
    ("_principal_generations", "principal"), ("_session_mint_bindings", "principal"),
];

/// What a development reset did. The caller unlinks `assets` after the
/// commit, outside any engine guard it holds.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct DevReset {
    pub previous_store_id: String,
    pub store_id: String,
    /// The store's head after the reset and any reseed.
    pub seq: u64,
    pub seeded: u64,
    pub assets: assets::AssetSweep,
}

impl Engine {
    /// Empty every application table and everything derived from them
    /// (views, maintained columns, indexes, counts, the partition log),
    /// every queue (effects, jobs, actions), uploaded assets and every
    /// non-service account and session; keep the adopted schema and
    /// generation. The store takes a new identity so a connected device
    /// re-acquires instead of keeping its partition; the head keeps counting
    /// (the reset is itself a commit). `rows` are then written as `/seed`
    /// writes them, in the same transaction. Development only: the release
    /// host never calls this.
    pub fn dev_reset_at(&mut self, rows: Vec<(String, Value)>, now: i64) -> Result<DevReset, RuntimeError> {
        // Like adoption: no ephemeral program runs across the identity change.
        drop(self.ephemeral.lock()?);
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#2-fixture-semantics — the new store identity must not inherit the retired store's future clock.
        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key = 'time_audience_clock'", [])?;
        let now = time_audience::pin_writer_time(&self.schema, &tx, now)?;
        let previous_store_id = tx.meta("store_id")?.unwrap_or_default();
        let head = tx.next_seq()?.saturating_sub(1);
        let assets: Vec<String> = tx.conn().prepare("SELECT id FROM _assets")?.query_map([], |row| row.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
        let tables: Vec<String> = tx.conn()
            .prepare("SELECT name FROM main.sqlite_schema WHERE type = 'table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite\\_%' ESCAPE '\\'")?
            .query_map([], |row| row.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.039#6.5 — every table empties together: no referential action runs, and no reference is left naming a removed row.
        for table in &tables {
            if KEPT.contains(&table.as_str()) { continue; }
            let quoted = format!("\"{}\"", table.replace('"', "\"\""));
            match SERVICE_SEATS.iter().find(|(name, _)| name == table) {
                Some((_, column)) => tx.conn().execute(&format!("DELETE FROM {quoted} WHERE substr({column}, 1, ?2) <> ?1"),
                    rusqlite::params![crate::identity::SERVICE_PREFIX, crate::identity::SERVICE_PREFIX.len() as i64])?,
                None => tx.conn().execute(&format!("DELETE FROM {quoted}"), [])?,
            };
        }
        // Prime at the reset clock in this transaction: no old period fires,
        // and the next crossed boundary fires normally. Replay's count restarts.
        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key LIKE 'schedule:%' OR key LIKE 'schedule-shape:%' OR key = 'dev:suppressed-effects'", [])?;
        for schedule in &self.schema.schedules {
            tx.set_meta(&format!("schedule:{}", schedule.name), &schedule.boundary(now).to_string())?;
            tx.set_meta(&format!("schedule-shape:{}", schedule.name), &crate::value::digest(schedule))?;
        }
        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _meta SET value = lower(hex(randomblob(16))) WHERE key = 'store_id'", [])?;
        let store_id = tx.meta("store_id")?.unwrap_or_default();
        // Every table changed: no change evidence may certify an interval
        // across the reset.
        tx.changes.borrow_mut().widen();
        let touched: Vec<String> = self.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, _)| name.clone()).collect();
        tx.record_commit(head + 1, now, "dev reset", crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL, "reset", &touched, 0)?;
        if crate::store::backfills::has_evidence(&tx)? {crate::store::backfills::prepare_inputs(&tx,&self.schema,None)?;}
        let seeded = seed_rows(&self.schema, &tx, rows, now)?;
        let seq = tx.next_seq()?.saturating_sub(1);
        tx.commit()?;
        // A new store identity starts a fresh bounded expiry walk. Clear only
        // after commit so a refused reset keeps the old store's continuation.
        self.expiry_after.clear();
        self.timeline_schedule = timeline::Schedule::default();
        self.ephemeral.rebind_committed(store_id.clone(), self.generation, &self.schema);
        Ok(DevReset { previous_store_id, store_id, seq, seeded, assets: assets::AssetSweep::named(assets, self.store.assets_dir()) })
    }
}

//! Release activation extends the existing engine transaction and generation.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — CAS includes every activation, not only semantic changes.
use super::*;
use rusqlite::OptionalExtension;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ReleaseActivation {
    pub release: u64,
    pub expected_previous: Option<u64>,
    pub expected_revision: Option<u64>,
    pub restore_dormant: bool,
    pub native_digest: Option<String>,
    pub operation: String,
}
impl ReleaseActivation {
    pub fn new(release: u64, expected_previous: Option<u64>) -> Self {
        Self {
            release,
            expected_previous,
            expected_revision: None,
            restore_dormant: true,
            native_digest: None,
            operation: "activate".into(),
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn check(&self, conn: &rusqlite::Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let actual: Option<String> = conn
            .query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release'", [], |r| {
                r.get(0)
            })
            .optional()?;
        if actual
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok() != self.expected_previous)
        {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("current release changed; run `snapback4 releases <dir>` and retry the selected release".into()));
        }
        let revision: Option<String> = conn
            .query_row(
                "SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release_revision'",
                [],
                |r| r.get(0),
            )
            .optional()?;
        let revision = revision.and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
        if self.expected_revision.is_some_and(|n| n != revision) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("activation revision changed (including rollback/equal-content activation); run `snapback4 releases <dir>` and retry".into()));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl Engine {
    pub fn new_release(
        store: Store,
        schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        release: u64,
        expected_previous: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new_release_with_feed_adoption(
            store,
            schema,
            programs,
            release,
            expected_previous,
            false,
        )
    }
    pub fn new_release_with_feed_adoption(
        store: Store,
        schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        release: u64,
        expected_previous: Option<u64>,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new_release_prepared(
            store,
            schema,
            programs,
            &ReleaseActivation::new(release, expected_previous),
            allow_feed_adoption,
        )
    }
    pub fn new_release_prepared(
        store: Store, schema: Schema, programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation, allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new_release_prepared_at(store,schema,programs,options,allow_feed_adoption,crate::now_ms())
    }
    pub fn new_release_prepared_at(
        mut store: Store,
        mut schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
        now: i64,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        prepare(&mut schema)?;
        let programs = programs.into_iter().map(|p| (p.name.clone(), p)).collect();
        let (generation, metadata) = Self::activate(
            &mut store,
            &schema,
            &programs,
            Some(options),
            now,
            allow_feed_adoption,
            None,
            None,
        )?;
        store.exclude_ephemeral(&schema);
        Ok(Self {
            timeline_schedule: timeline::Schedule::default(),
            ephemeral: std::sync::Arc::new(ephemeral::Hub::new(
                metadata.store_id,
                generation,
                &schema,
            )),
            store,
            schema,
            programs,
            generation,
            expiry_after: BTreeMap::new(),
            dev_effects: false,
            ceilings: Ceilings::default(),
            entropy: crate::ids::seed(),
            minter: crate::ids::Mint::default(),
            #[cfg(feature = "native")]
            native: None,
            #[cfg(feature = "native")]
            native_logs: native::Logs::default(),
            presence: None,
        })
    }
    /// No replacement engine, post-commit SQL read, or fallible ephemeral swap.
    pub fn adopt_release(
        &mut self, schema: Schema, programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation, allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    ) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.adopt_release_at(schema,programs,options,allow_feed_adoption,crate::now_ms())
    }
    pub fn adopt_release_at(
        &mut self,
        mut schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
        now: i64,
    ) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        prepare(&mut schema)?;
        let programs = programs.into_iter().map(|p| (p.name.clone(), p)).collect();
        drop(self.ephemeral.lock()?);
        let (generation, metadata) = Self::activate(
            &mut self.store,
            &schema,
            &programs,
            Some(options),
            now,
            allow_feed_adoption,
            None,
            None,
        )?;
        self.store.exclude_ephemeral(&schema);
        self.timeline_schedule = timeline::Schedule::default();
        self.schema = schema;
        self.programs = programs;
        self.generation = generation;
        self.expiry_after.clear();
        self.dev_effects = false;
        self.ephemeral
            .rebind_committed(metadata.store_id, generation, &self.schema);
        Ok(generation)
    }
    #[cfg(feature = "native")]
    pub fn set_prepared_native(&mut self, module: Option<std::sync::Arc<native::Module>>) {
        self.native = module;
    }
}

fn prepare(schema: &mut Schema) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    columns::validate_before_prepare(schema)?;
    schema.check_row_view_audiences()?;
    schema.prepare_contribution_indexes();
    schema.prepare_delivery_lookup();
    if let Some(issue) = crate::schema::validate(schema).first() {
        return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
            "{}: {}",
            issue.path, issue.message
        )));
    }
    Ok(())
}

//! Database-authoritative release history and explicit dormant pruning.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#4-explicit-pruning-and-audit — no implicit collector or unaudited data loss.
use super::*;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct DormantItem {
    /// Stable cursor; column/index names may coincide.
    pub key: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub kind: String,
    pub action: String,
    pub rows: u64,
    pub bytes: u64,
    pub asset_bytes: u64,
    /// Internal audit distinction: zero standalone cells may still be covered by a dormant row.
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub(crate) definition_only: bool,
    pub sample_ids: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub before_rows:Option<u64>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub after_rows:Option<u64>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub restored_rows:Option<u64>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub before_sample_ids:Option<Vec<String>>,
}

impl DormantItem {
    pub fn unit(&self) -> &'static str {
        match (self.kind.as_str(), self.rows) { ("backfill", 1) => "preimage", ("backfill", _) => "preimages", ("column", 1) => "cell", ("column", _) => "cells", ("index", 1) => "entry", ("index", _) => "entries", (_, 1) => "row", _ => "rows" }
    }
}

impl Store {
    /// Values presently in active rows, independently of table population or other columns.
    pub fn rollback_incompatible_values(&self, current: &Schema, target: &Schema, name: &str, column: &str) -> Result<(u64, Vec<String>), RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.authority_transaction()?;
        let table = &target.tables[name];
        let kind = &table.columns[column];
        if table.engine_field(column) || table.maintained.contains_key(column) { return Ok((0, Vec::new())); }
        let mut count = 0;
        let mut samples = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        let mut statement = tx.conn().prepare("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=(SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1) ORDER BY id")?;
        for data in statement.query_map([name], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))? {
            let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data?).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
            crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, &current.tables[name]);
            if let Ok(value) = row.field(column)
                && super::narrowed::incompatible_column(&tx, table, name, column, kind, value)? {
                count += 1;
                if samples.len() < 5 {
                    let text = match value { Value::String(s) => format!("'{}'", s.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('\'', "\\'").replace('\n', "\\n").replace('\r', "\\r")), _ => canonical_json(value) };
                    samples.insert(super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&text));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok((count, samples.into_iter().collect()))
    }
}

fn sql_number(n: u64) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
    i64::try_from(n).map_err(|_| {
        RuntimeError::Generation("release counter exceeds SQLite integer range".into())
    })
}

pub(super) fn present(conn: &Connection) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(conn.query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE type='table' AND name='_dormant_tables')",
        [],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?)
}
pub(crate) fn inventory(
    conn: &Connection,
    after: Option<&str>,
    limit: usize,
) -> Result<Vec<DormantItem>, RuntimeError> {
    if !present(conn)? {
        return Ok(Vec::new());
    }
    let narrowed = if super::narrowed::present(conn)? {
        " UNION ALL SELECT c.name,'rows',c.name,NULL FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=n.t GROUP BY n.t "
    } else {""};
    let backfills=super::backfills::inventory_sql(conn)?;
    let query=format!("SELECT path,kind,table_name,column_name FROM (
        SELECT table_name AS path,'table' AS kind,table_name,NULL AS column_name FROM _dormant_tables
        UNION ALL SELECT table_name||'.'||column_name,'column',table_name,column_name FROM _dormant_columns c WHERE table_name NOT IN (SELECT table_name FROM _dormant_tables) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values v JOIN _catalog t ON t.num=v.t WHERE t.name=c.table_name AND v.column_name=c.column_name)
        UNION ALL SELECT table_name||'.'||index_name,'index',table_name,index_name FROM _dormant_indexes {narrowed} {backfills})
        WHERE (?1 IS NULL OR kind||':'||path>?1) ORDER BY kind||':'||path LIMIT ?2");
    let mut statement=conn.prepare(&query)?;
    let items: Vec<(String, String, String, Option<String>)> = statement
        .query_map(
            params![after, i64::try_from(limit).unwrap_or(i64::MAX)],
            |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?, r.get(2)?, r.get(3)?)),
        )?
        .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for (path, kind, name, column) in items {
        let t: Option<i64> = conn
            .query_row("SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1", [&name], |r| {
                r.get(0)
            })
            .optional()?;
        let Some(t) = t else { continue };
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — dormant row images cover their retained cells; standalone cells have active owners.
        let (mut count,mut bytes):(u64,u64)=match kind.as_str(){
            "backfill"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(inputs)+octet_length(before_image)+octet_length(after_image)),0) FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE t=?1 AND definition=?2",params![t,column],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            "table"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(data)),0) FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1",[t],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            "rows"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(data)),0) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1",[t],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            "column"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(data)),0) FROM _dormant_values v WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id)",params![t,column],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            _=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),0 FROM (SELECT id FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1)",[t],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
        };
        if matches!(kind.as_str(), "table" | "rows") {
            let images = if kind == "table" { "_dormant_rows" } else { "_dormant_narrow_rows" };
            bytes += conn.query_row(&format!("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(v.id)+octet_length(v.data)),0) FROM _dormant_values v WHERE v.t=?1 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM {images} d WHERE d.t=v.t AND d.id=v.id)"), [t], |r| r.get::<_, i64>(0))? as u64;
        }
        let query = match kind.as_str() {
            "backfill" => "SELECT id FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE t=?1 AND definition=?2 ORDER BY id LIMIT 5",
            "rows" => "SELECT id FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND ?2 IS NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT 5",
            "table" => "SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND ?2 IS NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT 5",
            "column" => {
                "SELECT id FROM _dormant_values v WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id) ORDER BY id LIMIT 5"
            }
            _ => {
                "SELECT id FROM (SELECT id FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1) WHERE ?2 IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT 5"
            }
        };
        let mut ids = conn
            .prepare(query)?
            .query_map(params![t, column], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))?
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?
            .into_iter()
            .map(|id| super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&id))
            .collect();
        if kind == "index" {
            (count, ids) = index_entries(conn, t, &name, column.as_deref().unwrap())?;
        }
        let asset_bytes = if kind == "index" || kind == "backfill" {
            0
        } else {
            let selector=match kind.as_str(){
                "rows"=>"EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=?3 AND n.id=_assets.home_id)",
                "column"=>"EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values v JOIN r ON r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id WHERE v.t=?3 AND v.id=_assets.home_id AND v.column_name=_assets.home_column)",
                _=>"EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_rows d WHERE d.t=?3 AND d.id=_assets.home_id)",
            };
            conn.query_row(&format!("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(bytes),0) FROM _assets WHERE home_table=?1 AND (?2 IS NULL OR home_column=?2) AND released_at IS NULL AND {selector}"),params![name,if kind=="table" || kind=="rows" {None}else{column.as_deref()},t],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64))?
        };
        let definition_only = if kind == "column" {
            !conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2)", params![t, column], |r| r.get::<_, bool>(0))?
        } else { count == 0 };
        out.push(DormantItem {
            key: format!("{kind}:{path}"),
            path,
            kind,
            action: "dormant".into(),
            rows: count,
            bytes,
            asset_bytes,
            definition_only,
            sample_ids: ids,
            before_rows:None,after_rows:None,restored_rows:None,before_sample_ids:None,
        });
    }
    Ok(out)
}

/// Retained definitions represent logical entries, rebuilt on restoration.
/// A words index has zero or many entries per image; a row count is not its size.
fn index_entries(conn: &Connection, t: i64, table: &str, index: &str) -> Result<(u64, Vec<String>), RuntimeError> {
    let definition: String = conn.query_row("SELECT definition FROM _dormant_indexes WHERE table_name=?1 AND index_name=?2", params![table, index], |r| r.get(0))?;
    let index: crate::schema::Index = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
    let schema: Option<String> = conn.query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')", [], |r| r.get(0)).optional()?;
    let schema: Option<Schema> = schema.map(|s| serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))).transpose()?;
    let query = if super::narrowed::present(conn)? {
        "SELECT id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY id"
    } else { "SELECT id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY id" };
    let mut count = 0;
    let mut samples = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    let mut statement = conn.prepare(query)?;
    for image in statement.query_map([t], |r| Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?)))? {
        let (id, data) = image?;
        let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        if let Some(table) = schema.as_ref().and_then(|s| s.table(table)) { crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table); }
        for cell in conn.prepare("SELECT column_name,data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?.query_map(params![t, id], |r| Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?)))? {
            let (column, value) = cell?;
            if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row {
                fields.entry(column).or_insert(serde_json::from_str(&value).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?);
            }
        }
        let entries = crate::rows::index_keys(&index, &row, &id).len() as u64;
        count += entries;
        if entries > 0 && samples.len() < 5 { samples.insert(super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&id)); }
    }
    Ok((count, samples.into_iter().collect()))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#91-per-row-narrowed-value-dormancy — only rows surviving every activation phase count as restored.
pub(crate) fn finalize_restorations(tx:&Tx<'_>) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    if super::live_activation::active(tx)? { return super::live_activation::finalize(tx); }
    let entries=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT key,value FROM _meta WHERE key LIKE 'activation_narrowed_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_column_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_table_restored_ids:%'")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,String>(0)?,r.get::<_,String>(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
    let mut narrowed=0u64;
    for (key,ids) in entries {
        let (prefix,path)=key.split_once(':').expect("activation restoration key");
        let table=if prefix=="activation_column_restored_ids" {path.split_once('.').expect("column path").0} else {path};
        let count:u64=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?2))",params![tx.num(table)?,ids],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64))?;
        tx.set_meta(&format!("{}:{path}",prefix.trim_end_matches("_ids")),&count.to_string())?;
        if prefix=="activation_narrowed_restored_ids" {narrowed+=count;}
    }
    tx.set_meta("activation_narrowed_restored",&narrowed.to_string())?;
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn event(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    operation: &str,
    release: Option<u64>,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    summary: serde_json::Value,
    items: &[DormantItem],
) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    let revision = tx
        .meta("release_revision")?
        .and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0)
        .checked_add(1)
        .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Generation("release revision exhausted".into()))?;
    tx.set_meta("release_revision", &revision.to_string())?;
    let generation = tx
        .meta("generation")?
        .and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1);
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _release_events(at,operation,release,previous,generation,revision,summary) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6,?7)",params![crate::now_ms(),operation,release.map(sql_number).transpose()?,previous.map(sql_number).transpose()?,sql_number(generation)?,sql_number(revision)?,summary.to_string()])?;
    let id = tx.conn().last_insert_rowid();
    for item in items {
        tx.conn().execute(
            "INSERT INTO _release_event_items VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",
            params![id, item.key, canonical_json(item)],
        )?;
    }
    Ok(revision)
}

impl Store {
    pub fn dormant_inventory(
        &mut self,
        after: Option<&str>,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> Result<Vec<DormantItem>, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.read_transaction()?;
        let items = inventory(tx.conn(), after, limit)?;
        tx.rollback()?;
        Ok(items)
    }
    /// One snapshot: current identity, bounded history and storage inventories.
    pub fn release_status(
        &mut self,
        after: u64,
        items_after: Option<&str>,
        event_id: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<serde_json::Value, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.read_transaction()?;
        let mut events = Vec::new();
        let mut inventory_page = Vec::<serde_json::Value>::new();
        if present(tx.conn())? {
            let mut s=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT id,at,operation,release,previous,generation,revision,summary FROM _release_events WHERE id>?1 ORDER BY id LIMIT 16")?;
            let mut rows = s.query([sql_number(after)?])?;
            while let Some(r) = rows.next()? {
                events.push(serde_json::json!({"id":r.get::<_,i64>(0)?,"at":r.get::<_,i64>(1)?,"operation":r.get::<_,String>(2)?,"release":r.get::<_,Option<i64>>(3)?,"previous":r.get::<_,Option<i64>>(4)?,"generation":r.get::<_,i64>(5)?,"revision":r.get::<_,i64>(6)?,"summary":serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&r.get::<_,String>(7)?).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?}));
            }
            if let Some(event) = event_id {
                let mut s=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT detail FROM _release_event_items WHERE event=?1 AND (?2 IS NULL OR path>?2) ORDER BY path LIMIT 16")?;
                for item in s.query_map(params![sql_number(event)?, items_after], |r| {
                    r.get::<_, String>(0)
                })? {
                    inventory_page.push(
                        serde_json::from_str(&item?)
                            .map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?,
                    );
                }
            } else {
                inventory_page = inventory(tx.conn(), items_after, 16)?
                    .into_iter()
                    .map(|i| serde_json::to_value(i).unwrap())
                    .collect();
            }
        }
        let workers:Vec<(String,String)>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT key,value FROM _meta WHERE key='worker:status' OR key LIKE 'worker:status:%' ORDER BY key LIMIT 16")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
        let previous_active: Option<u64> = if present(tx.conn())? {tx.conn().query_row("SELECT previous FROM _release_events WHERE release IS NOT NULL AND previous != release AND operation != 'prune' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",[],|r|r.get::<_,Option<i64>>(0)).optional()?.flatten().map(|n|n as u64)} else {None};
        let result = serde_json::json!({"previous_active":previous_active,"release":tx.meta("release")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()),"revision":tx.meta("release_revision")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),"generation":tx.meta("generation")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(1),"native_digest":tx.meta("release_native_digest")?,"events_after":events.last().map(|e|e["id"].clone()),"events":events,"items_after":inventory_page.last().map(|i|i["key"].clone()),"items":inventory_page,"event":event_id,"workers":workers});
        tx.rollback()?;
        Ok(result)
    }
    pub fn prune_dormant(
        &mut self,
        confirm: bool,
        expected_revision: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<serde_json::Value, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.transaction()?;
        let held = tx
            .meta("release_revision")?
            .and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(0);
        if expected_revision.is_some_and(|n| n != held) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation(
                "release revision changed; run releases and retry prune".into(),
            ));
        }
        let items = inventory(tx.conn(), None, usize::MAX)?;
        if !confirm {
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(
                serde_json::json!({"pruned":false,"items":items,"rewrite":"snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive"}),
            );
        }
        if !present(tx.conn())? {
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(serde_json::json!({"pruned":true,"items":[],"revision":held}));
        }
        // Release only dormant homes; active application data is untouched.
        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?1) WHERE home_table IN (SELECT table_name FROM _dormant_tables) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values v JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=v.t WHERE c.name=_assets.home_table AND v.column_name=_assets.home_column AND v.id=_assets.home_id)",[crate::now_ms()])?;
        if super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?1) WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=n.t WHERE c.name=_assets.home_table AND n.id=_assets.home_id)",[crate::now_ms()])?;
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=_dormant_values.t AND n.id=_dormant_values.id)",[])?;
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_narrow_rows",[])?;
        }
        let revision = event(
            &tx,
            "prune",
            tx.meta("release")?.and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()),
            None,
            serde_json::json!({"dropped_items":items.len(),"bytes":items.iter().map(|i|i.bytes).sum::<u64>(),"asset_bytes":items.iter().map(|i|i.asset_bytes).sum::<u64>()}),
            &items,
        )?;
        let event_id = tx.conn().last_insert_rowid();
        super::backfills::prune(&tx)?;
        tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _dormant_values;DELETE FROM _dormant_rows;DELETE FROM _dormant_refs;DELETE FROM _dormant_columns;DELETE FROM _dormant_indexes;DELETE FROM _dormant_tables;")?;
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(serde_json::json!({"pruned":true,"items":items,"revision":revision,"event":event_id}))
    }
}

 succeeded in 0ms:
//! Human preview groups facts about one affected table/operation once.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#15-wave-15-preview-and-rollback-day5 — full evidence remains available in JSON and verbose output.
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
use snapback4_core::{Schema, ir::Program, store::DormantItem, successor::Step};
struct Group { warnings: Vec<String>, notes: Vec<String>, priority: u8 }
impl Default for Group { fn default() -> Self {Self {warnings:Vec::new(),notes:Vec::new(),priority:2}} }
fn table_prefix(text: &str, tables: &BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<String> {
    tables.iter().filter(|name|text.strip_prefix(name.as_str()).is_some_and(|tail|tail.is_empty() || tail.starts_with(['.',':',' ']))).max_by_key(|name|name.len()).cloned()
}
fn subject(text: &str, tables: &BTreeSet<String>, programs: &BTreeMap<String,Program>) -> String {
    // Read only generated subject positions, never arbitrary words inside an
    // authored predicate/refusal/value. Unknown formats remain release facts.
    if let Some(table)=table_prefix(text,tables) {return table;}
    for marker in ["table ","dependent ","backfill: "] {
        if let Some(tail)=text.strip_prefix(marker) && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    }
    let prefix=text.split(';').next().unwrap_or(text);
    if text.starts_with("queries/counts ") && let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" read ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    if text.starts_with("release ") && let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" safety dependencies on ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    if text.starts_with("rollback users get release ") {
        if let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" quotas on ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
        if let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once("'s ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    }
    if text.starts_with("release ") {
        if let Some(token)=prefix.split_whitespace().last() && let Some((owner,effect))=token.rsplit_once('.') {
            if effect=="require" {return format!("operation {owner}");}
            if tables.contains(owner) && matches!(effect,"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete") {return owner.into();}
        }
        for name in programs.keys() {
            if prefix.ends_with(&format!(" operation {name}")) {return format!("operation {name}");}
        }
    }
    "release behavior".into()
}
fn brief(text: &str) -> String {
    // Semicolons also separate authored guards and multiple cap declarations;
    // retaining the full fact avoids dropping their contracts or caveats.
    text.to_owned()
}
pub(super) fn print(current: Option<&Schema>, target: &Schema, programs: &BTreeMap<String,Program>, warnings: &[String], notes: &[String], steps: &[Step], items: &[DormantItem]) {
    let tables: BTreeSet<_>=target.tables.keys().chain(current.into_iter().flat_map(|s|s.tables.keys())).cloned().collect();
    let mut groups=BTreeMap::<String,Group>::new();
    for (warning, lines) in [(true,warnings),(false,notes)] {
        for line in lines {
            let group=groups.entry(subject(line,&tables,programs)).or_default();
            let priority=if line.contains("safety guard") || line.contains(".require;") {0} else if line.contains("confidentiality") || line.contains("safety rule") {1} else {2};
            group.priority=group.priority.min(priority);
            let text=if priority==0 {line.clone()} else {brief(line)};
            let dest=if warning {&mut group.warnings} else {&mut group.notes};
            if !dest.contains(&text) {dest.push(text);}
        }
    }
    for step in steps {
        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.").unwrap_or(&step.path);
        let key=table_prefix(path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||path.to_owned());
        if path.strip_prefix(&key).is_some_and(|tail|tail.starts_with(".indexes.")) {continue;}
        let group=groups.entry(key).or_default();
        // Keep each user-visible source change even when another fact already
        // names this table. Only index plumbing is verbose-only.
        let detail=brief(&format!("{}: {}",path.replace(".columns.","."),step.message));
        if !group.notes.contains(&detail) {group.notes.push(detail);}
    }
    let mut storage=BTreeMap::<String,[u64;3]>::new();
    for item in items.iter().filter(|item|item.rows>0 && item.kind!="backfill") {
        let name=table_prefix(&item.path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||item.path.clone());
        let slot=match item.kind.as_str() {"column"=>1,"index"=>2,_=>0};
        storage.entry(name).or_default()[slot]+=item.rows;
    }
    for (name,[rows,cells,indexes]) in storage {
        groups.entry(name).or_default().notes.push(format!("retained: {rows} dormant rows, {cells} cells, {indexes} index entries"));
    }
    for item in items.iter().filter(|item|item.kind=="backfill" && item.rows>0) {
        let name=table_prefix(&item.path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||item.path.clone());
        groups.entry(name).or_default().notes.push(format!("retained: {} private backfill preimages ({} bytes)",item.rows,item.bytes));
    }
    let mut groups=groups.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    groups.sort_by_key(|(name,g)|(g.priority,name.clone()));
    for (name,mut group) in groups {
        let warning=!group.warnings.is_empty();
        group.warnings.append(&mut group.notes);
        println!("  {} {name}: {}{}",if warning {"WARNING"} else {"note:"},group.warnings.join("; "),if warning {"; verify these cases or preserve the current behavior in a bridge release"} else {""});
    }
}

#[test]
fn authored_values_cannot_assign_a_guard_to_an_unrelated_table() {
    let tables=BTreeSet::from(["unrelated".to_owned(),"workspace.plans".to_owned()]);
    let line="release 1 loosens book.require; old: require title != 'unrelated' else STOP; new: allow; rollback users lose a current safety guard";
    assert_eq!(subject(line,&tables,&BTreeMap::new()),"operation book");
    assert_eq!(subject("workspace.plans.state: 2 rows become dormant",&tables,&BTreeMap::new()),"workspace.plans");
}

fn all_fifty_wave17_change_shapes_run_without_migration_programs(){
 let cases:Vec<Json>=serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../tests/backfills/wave17.json")).unwrap();assert_eq!(cases.len(),50);
 for case in cases {
  let lane=case["lane"].as_u64().unwrap();let inputs=case["inputs"].as_object().unwrap();let outputs=case["outputs"].as_object().unwrap();
  let columns=|fields:&serde_json::Map<String,Json>|fields.iter().map(|(c,t)|format!("  {c}: {}\n",t.as_str().unwrap())).collect::<String>();
  let old=format!("table rows:\n  legacy: bool default true\n{}  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n",columns(inputs));
  let args=inputs.iter().map(|(c,t)|format!("{c}: {}",t.as_str().unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
  let next=format!("table rows:\n  legacy: bool default true\n{}  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nbackfill rows change{lane}({args}):\n  {}\n",columns(outputs),case["expression"].as_str().unwrap());
  let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),next.clone())]);assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(),"lane {lane} {}: {:?}\n{next}",case["slug"],bound.diagnostics);
  let mut e=engine(&old);
  for (i,r) in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){let mut input=r["input"].as_object().unwrap().clone();input.insert("id".into(),json!(format!("r{i}")));seed(&mut e,"rows",Json::Object(input));}
  adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane}: {error}\n{next}"));
  assert!(e.schema().public_projection().backfills.is_empty(),"historical transforms never enter device input");
  for(i,r)in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){
   let got=row(&mut e,"rows",&format!("r{i}"));
   for(c,wanted)in r["expect"].as_object().unwrap(){assert_eq!(crate::wire::to_json(got.field(c).unwrap()),*wanted,"lane {lane} row {i} column {c}");}
  }
  adopt(&mut e,&old,1).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane} rollback: {error}"));
  for(i,r)in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){for(c,wanted)in r["input"].as_object().unwrap(){assert_eq!(crate::wire::to_json(&field(&mut e,"rows",&format!("r{i}"),c)),*wanted,"lane {lane} restored {c}");}}
  adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane} forward: {error}"));
 }
}

fn unique_lookup_uses_historical_rows_and_rechecks_changed_lookup_values(){
 let old="table members:\n  principal: principal\n  plot: text <=24\n  unique principal\n  read <- deny\n  online only\ntable bookings:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  owner: principal","  plot: text <=24\n  owner: principal")+"backfill bookings plot(owner: principal):\n  {plot:(members[principal = owner] ! else NOT_FOUND).plot}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":"m","principal":"user:a","plot":"A17"}));seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"a","owner":"user:a"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","plot"),Value::String("A17".into()));
 adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();
 // Trusted seed replaces the source row as an operator update for this test.
 seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":"m","principal":"user:a","plot":"B20"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","plot"),Value::String("B20".into()));

fn cross_reference_existence_is_evaluated_before_any_table_output_changes(){

 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","a","confidence"),Value::String("confirmed".into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","b","confidence"),Value::String("confirmed".into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","c","confidence"),Value::String("possible".into()));

#[test]
fn retained_table_reentry_never_feeds_a_transform_its_own_output(){
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 for direct_old in [false,true] {
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();

#[test]
fn partially_hidden_transformed_fields_resume_their_version_and_undo_on_plain_return() {

  adopt(&mut e,&hidden,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","other"),Value::Int(8));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(if edited {11}else{6}));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","other"),Value::Int(9));
  adopt(&mut e,&hidden,3).unwrap();
  if edited {let error=adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("changed after backfill"),"{error}");}
  else {adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(7));}
 }
}

    /// How many entries sit under `prefix`, counting at most `limit`.
    pub fn count_prefix(&self, table: &str, index_name: &str, prefix: &[Value], limit: u64) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        let ti = self.num(&format!("{table}.{index_name}"))?;
        let base = key::encode(prefix);
        let hi = key::upper_bound(&base);
        let count: i64 = self.tx.prepare_cached("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (SELECT 1 FROM i WHERE ti = ?1 AND k >= ?2 AND k < ?3 LIMIT ?4)")?.query_row(params![ti, base, hi, limit as i64], |row| row.get(0))?;
        Ok(count as u64)
    }

    /// Write a row image: a fresh insert when `old` is none, else a replace
    /// that touches only the index entries whose keys changed (a `words`
    /// index has one per token). Returns the number of entries written.
    pub fn put(&self, table_name: &str, table: &Table, row: &Value, old: Option<&Value>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.put_indexed(table_name, table, row, old, "INSERT INTO i(ti, k, id) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)")
    }

    /// A replacement loader has already cleared the rows and created its
    /// temporary index heap. It must install those indexes before any read.
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.4 — native replacement sorts complete indexes inside the publication transaction.
    pub fn put_loading_indexes(&self, table_name: &str, table: &Table, row: &Value) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.put_indexed(table_name, table, row, None, "INSERT INTO temp._device_index_load(ti, k, id) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)")
    }

    fn put_indexed(&self, table_name: &str, table: &Table, row: &Value, old: Option<&Value>, insert_index: &str) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.clear_activation_scan();
        if table.ephemeral.is_some() { return Err(RuntimeError::Store("durable transaction cannot write an ephemeral table".into())); }
        let t = self.num(table_name)?;
        let id = row.field("id")?.as_str()?;
        narrowed::check_write(self,table_name,table,row,old)?;
        backfills::observe_write(self,t,id,table,row,old)?;
        let keys = crate::rows::index_write(table_name, table, row, old, id)?;
        self.changes.borrow_mut().record(table_name, table, old, Some(row));
        let data = canonical_json(row);
        let sql = if old.is_some() { "UPDATE r SET data = ?3 WHERE t = ?1 AND id = ?2" } else { "INSERT INTO r(t, id, data) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)" };
        self.tx.prepare_cached(sql)?.execute(params![t, id, data])?;
        let mut insert = self.tx.prepare_cached(insert_index)?;
        let mut entries = 0;
        for crate::rows::IndexWrite { name, before, after } in keys {
            if before == after { continue; }
            let ti = self.num(&format!("{table_name}.{name}"))?;
            for key in before.iter().filter(|key| !after.contains(key)) {
                self.conn().prepare_cached("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti = ?1 AND k = ?2")?.execute(params![ti, key])?;
                entries += 1;
            }
            for key in after.iter().filter(|key| !before.contains(key)) {
                insert.execute(params![ti, key, id])?;
                entries += 1;
            }
        }
        entries += self.update_cap_counts(table_name, table, old, Some(row))?;
        Ok(entries)
    }

    pub fn delete(&self, table_name: &str, table: &Table, old: &Value) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.clear_activation_scan();
        if table.ephemeral.is_some() { return Err(RuntimeError::Store("durable transaction cannot write an ephemeral table".into())); }
        let t = self.num(table_name)?;
        self.changes.borrow_mut().record(table_name, table, Some(old), None);
        let id = old.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
        dormant::delete(self,t,&id,table_name)?;
        self.conn().prepare_cached("DELETE FROM r WHERE t = ?1 AND id = ?2")?.execute(params![t, id])?;
        let mut entries = 0;
        for (index_name, index) in &table.indexes {
            let ti = self.num(&format!("{table_name}.{index_name}"))?;
            for key in stored_index_keys(table, index, old, &id) {
                self.conn().prepare_cached("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti = ?1 AND k = ?2")?.execute(params![ti, key])?;
                entries += 1;
            }
        }
        entries += self.update_cap_counts(table_name, table, Some(old), None)?;
        Ok(entries)
    }

    pub fn next_seq(&self) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        Ok(self.tx.prepare_cached("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(seq), 0) + 1 FROM _commits")?.query_row([], |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0))? as u64)
    }

    /// A meta key written with the transaction (a device's watermark lands
    /// with the rows it covers).
    pub fn set_meta(&self, key: &str, value: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _meta(key, value) VALUES (?1, ?2) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value", params![key, value])?;
