//! Writes: the four words, their rules, the constraints (unique, cap,
//! immutable, append-only, after-insert), maintained aggregates, and the
//! partition events every row change produces.

use super::runner::Runner;
use super::validate::validate_row;
use crate::error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError};
use crate::partition;
use crate::rows::index_values;
use crate::rows::{Bounds, Dir};
use crate::schema::{Constraint, Effect, Table, materialize_optional_fields};
use crate::value::{Supplied, Value, canonical_json};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum Origin {
    Source,
    Maintained,
    LiveAuthority,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub(super) struct FinalExistsWitness {
    table: String,
    row: Value,
    constraint: usize,
}

#[path = "write_references.rs"]
mod references;
#[path = "write_target.rs"]
pub(crate) mod target;

impl Runner<'_> {
    pub(super) fn insert(&mut self, table: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.insert_mode(table, row, false).map(|_| ())
    }

    pub(super) fn insert_if_absent(&mut self, table: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        self.insert_mode(table, row, true)
    }

    fn insert_mode(&mut self, table_name: &str, row: Supplied, if_absent: bool) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        if self.device && self.schema.tables[table_name].not_before.is_some() {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(
                "a mutation touching a `not before` table runs on the server".into(),
            ));
        }
        if self.device && if_absent && self.schema.tables[table_name].through.is_some() {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("insert if absent on a read-through child queues without a row".into()));
        }
        self.target_attempt(table_name, |runner| {
            let mark = runner.meter.mark();
            let result = runner.insert_attempt(table_name, row, if_absent, None).map_err(|error| error.authorization_context(table_name, Effect::Insert));
            // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — denied direct allocations hide deferred
            // read witnesses and counter work just as NOT_FOUND hides targets.
            if matches!(&result, Err(RuntimeError::Rule { effect: Effect::Insert, .. } | RuntimeError::AuthorizationBudget { .. }))
                && runner.schema.tables.get(table_name).is_some_and(|table| !table.sequences.is_empty()) {
                runner.meter.restore(mark);
            }
            result
        })
    }

    fn insert_attempt(&mut self, table_name: &str, row: Supplied, if_absent: bool, upsert_index: Option<&str>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        self.refuse_engine_owned(table_name, Effect::Insert)?;
        self.refuse_receipt_fields(table_name, &row, true)?;
        self.refuse_recorded(table_name, &row)?;
        let supplied_sequence = super::sequences::refuse_fields(&self.schema.tables[table_name], table_name, &row);
        // A directly supplied field is a payload error before any lookup;
        // only an upsert's copied address waits for insert authority.
        if upsert_index.is_none() && supplied_sequence.is_err() { return supplied_sequence.map(|_| false); }
        let supplied = row.clone();
        let row = self.with_defaults(table_name, row)?;
        if self.schema.tables[table_name].ephemeral.is_some() { self.meter.heap(row.heap_bytes())?; }
        let row = row.ephemeral(&self.schema.tables[table_name], self.context.now.unwrap_or(0))?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.014#4-the-capture — reserve, then stamp the parent capture before any rule sees the image.
        self.reserve_capture(&self.schema.tables[table_name])?;
        let row = self.capture_insert(table_name, row)?;
        let table = &self.schema.tables[table_name];
        let independent = !table.sequences.is_empty() && !super::rules::deciding_fields(table_name, table, &table.rules.insert)
            .iter().any(|field| table.sequences.contains_key(field));
        // An independent denial must never consult a hidden read witness.
        let authorized = independent && self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)?;
        if independent && !authorized {
            return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "insert rule") });
        }
        if !table.sequences.is_empty() {
            // Admission reserves the worst case before either read verdict;
            // restoring a refusal alone cannot hide a boundary abort.
            // An independent insert rule was already prepaid and evaluated.
            // Only still-pending checks belong in this preflight allowance.
            let insert_checks = if authorized { 0 } else { 1 + u64::from(upsert_index.is_some() && supplied_sequence.is_ok()) };
            self.reserve_sequence_authorization(table, &row, insert_checks)?;
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — an unreadable allocation cannot expose counter,
        // uniqueness or cap state, even to a principal allowed to insert.
        if !self.sequence_insert_readable(table_name, &row)? {
            self.refusing_clause(table_name, "sequence read authority");
            return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "sequence read authority") });
        }
        if let Err(error) = supplied_sequence {
            // @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — absent upserts decide insert authority before field diagnostics.
            // A target key may supply an ordinal even when the patch does not.
            if !authorized && !self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)? {
                return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "insert rule") });
            }
            return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { error });
        }
        if upsert_index.is_some() {
            self.authorize_fields(table_name, &row, &supplied).map_err(|_| self.no_write_target())?;
        } else { self.authorize_fields(table_name, &row, &supplied)?; }
        // The image copy an event slot costs is the writer's own supplied bytes (LLP 3000.015 §1).
        let image_bytes = row.heap_bytes();
        let (row, sequence_full) = self.sequence_fields(table_name, row)?;
        let row = self.receipt_insert_fields(table_name, row)?;
        if let Some(index) = upsert_index {
            if self.device { self.prove_upsert_insert(table_name, index, &row)?; }
            // Preserve upsert's target preflight before the ordinary insert checks;
            // only this first authority refusal is an undisclosed target miss.
            if !authorized && !self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)? { return Err(self.no_write_target()); }
            if sequence_full { return Err(super::sequences::exhausted()); }
            // Engine event slots are stamped after predecessor/sequence checks,
            // just as on an ordinary insert; preflight validates authored fields.
            if let Err(error) = super::validate::validate_before_slots(self.schema, table_name, &row) {
                return Err(if matches!(error, RuntimeError::Input(_)) && !self.context.is_system() { self.no_write_target() } else { error });
            }
        }
        if !authorized && !self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)? {
            return Err(self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "insert rule"));
        }
        // Retry keys independent of engine allocation still identify a no-op
        // at the ceiling. Never use the clamped authority candidate as a key.
        if sequence_full && if_absent {
            let table = &self.schema.tables[table_name];
            for (name, index) in table.indexes.iter().filter(|(_, index)| {
                index.unique && index.columns().iter().all(|column| !table.sequences.contains_key(column))
            }) {
                let key = index_values(index, &row);
                if key.contains(&Value::Null) { continue; }
                if self.collision_lookup(table_name, name, index, &key, None, Effect::Insert)?.is_some() {
                    self.observe_sequence_columns(table_name, &row, &index.columns(), true)?;
                    return Ok(false);
                }
            }
        }
        if sequence_full { return Err(super::sequences::exhausted()); }
        let row = self.record_insert(table_name, row)?;
        let row = self.with_asset_facts(table_name, row, None, None)?;
        if if_absent {
            for (name, index) in table.indexes.iter().filter(|(_, index)| index.unique) {
                let key = index_values(index, &row);
                if key.iter().any(|value| *value == Value::Null) {
                    continue;
                }
                if self.collision_lookup(table_name, name, index, &key, None, Effect::Insert)?.is_some() {
                    self.observe_sequence_columns(table_name, &row, &index.columns(), true)?;
                    return Ok(false);
                }
            }
        }
        let id = row.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.5 — if-absent's insert arm needs the same image, unique-population and cap proof as upsert.
        // Upsert mints its insert id; if-absent may instead supply one.
        // Only a freshly minted id can skip the primary-key absence proof.
        let minted = self.context.minted_ids.contains(&id);
        let proof = if !self.device { None } else if minted {
            table.indexes.iter().find(|(name, index)| {
                    name.as_str() != "byId" && index.unique && !index_values(index, &row).contains(&Value::Null)
                })
                .map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).or_else(|| {
                    table.constraints.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Cap { .. })).then_some("byId")
                })
        } else { Some("byId") };
        if if_absent && let Some(index) = proof.or(self.device.then_some("byId")) {
            self.prove_upsert_insert(table_name, index, &row)?;
        }
        // An upsert has already resolved this exact address (or minted the id)
        // and reserved both target proofs. Retiring an expired predecessor
        // uses that same allowance, not a third read-rule reservation.
        let collision_probes = if upsert_index.is_some() { 0 } else { super::rules::rule_probes(&table.rules.read) };
        let has_through_children = self.schema.children(table_name).next().is_some();
        let has_children = has_through_children || self.schema.is_referenced(table_name);
        let predecessor = self.authorized_scan(&[(MeterKind::RuleProbes, collision_probes)], |runner, allowance| {
            let existing = runner.tx.get(allowance, table_name, &id)?;
            if let Some(row) = &existing {
                if !runner.expired(table, row) { runner.visible_collision(table_name, row, Effect::Insert, "unique id")?; }
                // An expired predecessor's read rule cannot veto actionable
                // reference retirement. A declared read-through child is
                // different: a non-reader cannot probe whether hidden
                // physical children retain the predecessor.
                else if has_through_children && !runner.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Read, None, row)? {
                    return Err(runner.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "unique id"));
                }
            }
            Ok(existing)
        })?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.039#6.4 — a logical device miss cannot prove there is
        // no hidden, expired physical predecessor whose retirement would run
        // reference actions. Controller-minted identities are the existing
        // fresh-address proof; every other referenced same-id insert queues.
        if self.device && predecessor.is_none() && has_children && table.is_expiring() && !minted {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(format!(
                "same-id insert into referenced {table_name} needs the server's physical predecessor check; queued without predicted rows"
            )));
        }
        if predecessor.as_ref().is_none_or(|old| self.expired(table, old)) && has_children {
            let mark = self.meter.mark();
            if self.physical_children(table_name, &Value::Id(id.clone()))? {
                // The bounded probes have completed. Their occupancy is not
                // disclosed by the collision's logical meter or heap peak.
                self.meter.restore(mark);
                return Err(if predecessor.is_some() {
                    RuntimeError::Constraint(format!("{table_name} already has a row with id {id}"))
                } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "parent identity has stored children") });
            }
        }
        if let Some(existing) = &predecessor {
            if !self.expired(table, &existing) {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Constraint(format!("{table_name} already has a row with id {id}")));
            }
            // The expired predecessor is internal authorization work, not a
            // disclosed examination (LLP 3000.015 §§1–2).
            self.retire_expired_row(table_name, existing)?;
        }
        // Retiring an expired predecessor can cascade a source row whose
        // contribution determines this image's maintained columns. Initialize
        // them from the post-retirement private totals. Maintained columns are
        // forbidden from constraints and authority, so the earlier collision
        // and authorization preflights need only the authored image.
        let row = self.birth_columns(table_name, row)?;
        super::validate::validate_before_slots(self.schema, table_name, &row)?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.5 — an authored plain insert predicts unproved; a later refusal is then the server's
        // to report. The engine's own timeline writes are not the author's prediction.
        if let Some(index) = proof.filter(|_| {
            !if_absent && upsert_index.is_none() && !self.unproved_write && self.timeline_authority.is_none()
        }) {
            // An earlier unknown fact already makes this prediction unproved.
            // Otherwise the proof's own unknown fact is not the prediction's.
            self.unproved_write = self.missing_fact.is_some();
            if !self.unproved_write {
                let beyond = self.beyond_horizon;
                let proved = self.device_work(|runner| runner.prove_upsert_insert(table_name, index, &row));
                (self.beyond_horizon, self.missing_fact) = (beyond, None);
                match proved { Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(_)) => self.unproved_write = true, other => other? }
            }
        }
        // Retirement released every logical key and cap slot. Treat the new
        // image as a fresh insert: an unchanged secondary key can now belong
        // to another live row and must be probed again.
        self.check_constraints(table_name, None, &row, Effect::Insert)?;
        self.commit_sequences(table_name, &row)?;
        self.commit_receipt_counter(table_name, &row)?;
        let row = self.prepare_event_slots(table_name, None, row, image_bytes)?;
        validate_row(self.schema, table_name, &row)?;
        let entries = self.maintain_scratch(self.contribution_heap(table), |runner| {

exec

        self.charge_source_indexes(table, Some(&old), Some(&new), entries)?;
        self.row_changed(table_name, Some(&old), Some(&new), Origin::Source)
    }

    pub(super) fn delete(&mut self, table_name: &str, id: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if self.device && self.schema.tables[table_name].not_before.is_some() {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(
                "a mutation touching a `not before` table runs on the server".into(),
            ));
        }
        self.target_attempt(table_name, |runner| runner.delete_attempt(table_name, id))
    }

    fn delete_attempt(&mut self, table_name: &str, id: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.refuse_engine_owned(table_name, Effect::Delete)?;
        self.refuse_receipt_scope_delete(table_name)?;
        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
        self.reserve_write_rules(table, &[Effect::Read, Effect::Delete])?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9 — delete prepays hidden contribution work before fetching its old image, with the same attempt lifetime as update.
        self.reserve_maintains(table_name, table)?;
        let Some(old) = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| {
            runner.fetch_write_target(allowance, table_name, id)
        })? else {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Require { site: Some(self.statement_site.clone()), code: "NOT_FOUND".to_owned(), message: None });
        };
        // @ref LLP 3000.039#3 — a delete that consults referencing rows needs read authority on its row first.
        let readable =
            !self.inactive_write_target(table_name, &old)? && self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Read, None, &old)?;
        if (self.schema.children(table_name).next().is_some() || self.schema.is_referenced(table_name)) && !readable
        {
            self.refusing_clause(table_name, "read rule");
            return Err(self.no_write_target());
        }
        if !self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Delete, None, &old)? {
            return Err(if readable {
                RuntimeError::Rule { table: table_name.to_owned(), effect: Effect::Delete }
            } else { self.no_write_target() });
        }
        self.delete_authorized(table_name, old)
    }

    pub(super) fn delete_authorized(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let old = if self.schema.is_referenced(table_name) {
            self.apply_reference_actions(table_name, &old)?;
            // @ref LLP 3000.039#3 — the actions may have changed the root (a maintained column mirrored from its children): remove its current image.
            let id = old.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
            self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, table_name, &id))?.unwrap_or(old)
        } else { old };
        self.delete_row(table_name, old)
    }

    /// A delete's own removal of one row, its rules already decided: receipt
    /// membership, the read-through child probe, constraints, maintains and
    /// the partition event. A referential cascade removes each row this way.
    pub(super) fn delete_row(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let table = &self.schema.tables[table_name];
        self.receipt_member_removed(table_name, &old)?;
        self.refuse_live_children(table_name,old.field("id")?)?;
        self.check_constraints(table_name, Some(&old), &old, Effect::Delete)?;
        let entries = self.maintain_scratch(self.contribution_heap(table), |runner| {
            runner.tx.delete(table_name, table, &old)
        })?;
        self.charge_source_indexes(table, Some(&old), None, entries)?;
        self.row_changed(table_name, Some(&old), None, Origin::Source)
    }

    /// Remove a row the engine itself retires (an expired one): no rule and
    /// no constraint stands between the engine and its own sweep, while
    /// aggregates and partition events apply as for any delete.
    pub(super) fn remove(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: &Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.refuse_engine_owned(table_name, Effect::Delete)?;
        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
        let entries = self.maintain_scratch(self.contribution_heap(table), |runner| {
            runner.tx.delete(table_name, table, old)
        })?;
        self.charge_source_indexes(table, Some(old), None, entries)?;
        self.row_changed(table_name, Some(old), None, Origin::Source)
    }

    /// Physically retire one row whose upper lifetime ended. Expiry bypasses
    /// the root's authored delete rule and constraints, but it does not bypass
    /// the schema's declared effects on rows that reference the root. A
    /// read-through child still retains the expired parent physically.
    // @ref LLP 3000.039#3 — expiry cleanup runs the same closure/restrict/set-null actions as an authored delete.
    pub(crate) fn retire_expired_row(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        if self.physical_children(table_name, old.field("id")?)? { return Ok(false); }
        let current = if self.schema.is_referenced(table_name) {
            self.apply_reference_actions(table_name, old)?;
            // A cascade may maintain a column on the root; delete its current
            // physical image so index and cap entries match what is stored.
            let id = old.field("id")?.as_str()?;
            self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, table_name, id))?
                .unwrap_or_else(|| old.clone())
        } else {
            old.clone()
        };
        self.remove(table_name, &current)?;
        Ok(true)
    }

    /// At a program commit's end, retire every source row the commit changed
    /// or inserted whose lifetime has already ended: its final
    /// image was never live at a commit boundary, so it leaves no row for a
    /// later sweep to announce. Inside the commit the
    /// row existed as written (an insert-then-update still works). Engine
    /// bookkeeping, unmetered: the program's metering is already recorded.
    /// Server execution and device prediction both run it so a predicted
    /// final image cannot omit cascade, set-null or restrict effects. Its work,
    /// returned, stays
    /// linear in the rows retired: each aggregate row takes one net change
    /// and each audience key is scanned for a live row once. Seeds and loads
    /// ordinarily keep their rows for the sweep; row-keyed view sources use
    /// `retire_seeded_view_sources` to preserve their count-basis contract.
    // @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — a row whose lifetime ended by its own commit's end never exists at a commit boundary.
    pub(crate) fn retire_expired_births(&mut self) -> Result<crate::meter::Metered, RuntimeError> {
        let Some(now) = self.context.now else { return Ok(Default::default());
        };
        // @ref LLP 3000.036.000.000#3 — an expired final image never grants a new recipient the row's id.
        // The tracker covers synced and unsynced source tables alike. Each
        // distinct entry prepaid its tree node, strings and this vector slot;
        // the rows-written ceiling bounds the commit-end walk.
        // A row that existed before the commit and ends it already expired is
        // retired too: accounted from the partitions it was in at the commit's
        // start, it revokes there and announces nothing to a partition it only
        // entered with its expiring image.
        let mut changed: Vec<(String, String)> = std::mem::take(&mut self.expiry_rows).into_iter().collect();
        if changed.is_empty() {
            return Ok(Default::default());
        }
        // The log's order must not depend on a map's iteration order.
        let order = self.schema.through_order();
        changed.sort_by_key(|(name,id)| {
            (std::cmp::Reverse(order.iter().position(|n| n == name)),id.clone(),
            )
        });
        // Server execution already reserved this bookkeeping in the source
        // writes. Device prediction instead runs the walk inside its separate
        // bounded physical-work meter: exhausting that allowance queues the
        // intent, never publishes a partial closure or refuses the write.
        let meter = if self.device_work_active { None } else {
            Some(std::mem::replace(&mut self.meter, crate::meter::Meter::new(crate::meter::Ceilings::unbounded(), crate::meter::Cut::Abort)))
        };
        self.retiring = Some(Default::default());
        let retired = changed.iter().try_for_each(|(table_name, id)| {
            let Some(row) = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, table_name, id))? else { return Ok(());
            };
            let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
            if table.expired(&row, now) { self.retire_expired_row(table_name, &row).map(|_| ()) } else { Ok(()) }
        });
        let aggregates = self.retiring.take().map(|retiring| retiring.aggregates).unwrap_or_default();
        let retired = retired.and_then(|()| self.apply_retired_aggregates(aggregates));
        let work = meter.map(|meter| std::mem::replace(&mut self.meter, meter).used).unwrap_or_default();
        retired.map(|()| work)
    }

    /// Seed writes historically retire only already-expired sources of
    /// row-keyed views. Keep that narrow boundary while routing their removal
    /// through the common action and net-maintenance path.
    // @ref LLP 3000.039#6.5 — ordinary expired fixtures remain stored; derived row-view sources cannot revive after clock rollback.
    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    pub(crate) fn retire_seeded_view_sources(&mut self) -> Result<crate::meter::Metered, RuntimeError> {
        let schema = self.schema;
        self.expiry_rows.retain(|(table, _)| schema.maintains.iter()
            .any(|maintain| maintain.source == table.as_str() && maintain.row_key.is_some()));
        self.retire_expired_births()
    }

    /// A column with a declared default takes it when the written row leaves
    /// it out, before validation and before the asset facts, so the row the
    /// rules and the constraints see is the complete one.
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.4 — omitted defaults use the clock and viewer of the host running the insert.
    pub(super) fn with_defaults(&mut self, table_name: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<Supplied, RuntimeError> {
        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
        if !table.defaults.keys().any(|name| {
            row.as_object().is_ok_and(|fields| !fields.contains_key(name))
        }) {
            return Ok(row);
        }
        let row = row.defaults(table,self.context.now.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Input("write clock is not pinned".into()))?,&self.context.viewer)?;
        self.meter.heap(row.heap_bytes())?;
        Ok(row)
    }

    /// facts the server established about it (type, bytes, dimensions,
    /// duration), never the client's word for them. The upload must be the

//! The generation barrier checks predecessor audiences before destructive projection.
// @ref LLP 3000.014#7-activation — pinned time, child scan, capture before validation, atomic publication.
use crate::{
    error::RuntimeError,
    rows::{Bounds, Dir, Rows},
    schema::{RuleExpr, Schema, Table, through::PARENT_DEADLINE},
    value::Value,
};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

const EXPIRY_TABLE_CURSOR: &str = "$expiry-table";

/// Rotate a sweep's table order after the last table that consumed a bounded
/// visit. Per-table row cursors alone can starve every later table when an
/// earlier table contains a permanently restricted due row.
pub(crate) fn resume_expiry_tables(
    mut tables: Vec<String>,
    cursors: &BTreeMap<String, Vec<Value>>,
) -> Vec<String> {
    let after = cursors
        .get(EXPIRY_TABLE_CURSOR)
        .and_then(|cursor| cursor.first())
        .and_then(|value| value.as_str().ok());
    if let Some(index) = after.and_then(|after| tables.iter().position(|table| table == after)) {
        let next = (index + 1) % tables.len().max(1);
        tables.rotate_left(next);
    }
    tables
}

pub(crate) fn mark_expiry_table(cursors: &mut BTreeMap<String, Vec<Value>>, table: &str) {
    cursors.insert(EXPIRY_TABLE_CURSOR.into(), vec![Value::String(table.into())]);
}

/// Only inherited batches need staging. Preserve the streaming path for
/// existing schemas, including large fixture imports.
pub(crate) fn ancestor_order<'a, I>(
    schema: &Schema,
    rows: I,
) -> Box<dyn Iterator<Item = (String, Value)> + 'a>
where
    I: IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>,
    I::IntoIter: 'a,
{
    if schema.tables.values().all(|t| t.through.is_none()) {
        return Box::new(rows.into_iter());
    }
    let order = schema.through_order();
    let mut rows: Vec<_> = rows.into_iter().collect();
    rows.sort_by_key(|(name, _)| order.iter().position(|n| n == name).unwrap_or(usize::MAX));
    Box::new(rows.into_iter())
}

fn refusal(name: &str, count: usize, reason: &str, samples: &[String]) -> RuntimeError {
    RuntimeError::Structural {
        code: "E_READ_THROUGH_ADOPT",
        message: format!(
            "{name}: {count} child rows cannot adopt read through: {reason}; sample ids/parent values [{}]; rewrite: reconcile the named audience fields and live parents in a bridge release, then `snapback4 deploy <dir> --restore-dormant` (guide rules)",
            samples.join(", ")
        ),
    }
}

fn sample(samples: &mut Vec<String>, row: &Value, reference: &str, parent: &str) {
    fn bounded(text: &str) -> &str {
        let mut end = text.len().min(256);
        while !text.is_char_boundary(end) {
            end -= 1;
        }
        &text[..end]
    }
    if samples.len() < 5 {
        let id = row
            .field("id")
            .ok()
            .and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok())
            .unwrap_or("<missing>");
        let value = row
            .field(reference)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok())
            .unwrap_or("<missing>");
        samples.push(format!(
            "{}: {reference} -> {parent}[{}]",
            bounded(id),
            bounded(value)
        ));
    }
}

fn live_work(tx:&dyn Rows,name:&str,old:Option<&Table>,next:&Schema)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    Ok(old.and_then(|t|t.through.as_ref()).is_some_and(|t|t.live.is_some())
        || next.table(name).and_then(|t|t.through.as_ref()).is_some_and(|t|t.live.is_some())
        || tx.has_restored_live_authority(name)?)
}

// Both definitions may coexist in a raw source image until projection.
fn parent_bytes(a:Option<&Table>,b:Option<&Table>)->u64 {
    a.into_iter().chain(b).map(crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation).fold(0_u64,u64::saturating_add).saturating_mul(4)
}

fn rows(
    tx: &dyn Rows, name: &str, table: &Table, parent_scratch: u64, bounded: bool, destination: Option<&Table>,
    mut visit: impl FnMut(Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if !bounded {
        let mut bounds=Bounds::default();
        loop {
            let page=tx.scan(name,"byId",&[],&bounds,Dir::Asc,256)?;
            if page.is_empty() {return Ok(());}
            for (row,key) in page {bounds.after=crate::key::decode(&key);visit(row)?;}
        }
    }
    let decode=crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table);
    let keys=crate::rows::adoption::index_scratch(name,table).saturating_add(destination.map_or(0,|t|crate::rows::adoption::index_scratch(name,t)));
    let entries=crate::rows::adoption::index_capacity(table).saturating_add(destination.map_or(0,crate::rows::adoption::index_capacity)).saturating_mul(2);
    let scratch=decode.saturating_mul(4).saturating_add(destination.map_or(0,crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation).saturating_mul(4)).saturating_add(keys.saturating_mul(2))
        .saturating_add(parent_scratch).saturating_add(8192);
    tx.walk_adoption(name,decode,scratch,[2,table.columns.len() as u64+16,entries],&mut visit)
}

pub(crate) fn correspondence(
    child: &RuleExpr,
    parent: &RuleExpr,
) -> Option<std::collections::BTreeMap<String, String>> {
    if child.is_uniformly_readable() && parent.is_uniformly_readable() {
        return Some(Default::default());
    }
    fn walk(
        a: &serde_json::Value,
        b: &serde_json::Value,
        map: &mut std::collections::BTreeMap<String, String>,
    ) -> bool {
        use serde_json::Value::*;
        match (a, b) {
            (Object(a), Object(b)) if a.len() == b.len() => {
                if let (Some(String(a)), Some(String(b))) = (a.get("Field"), b.get("Field")) {
                    if map.get(a).is_some_and(|old| old != b)
                        || map.iter().any(|(old, v)| v == b && old != a)
                    {
                        return false;
                    }
                    map.insert(a.clone(), b.clone());
                    true
                } else {
                    a.iter()
                        .all(|(k, a)| b.get(k).is_some_and(|b| walk(a, b, map)))
                }
            }
            (Array(a), Array(b)) if a.len() == b.len() => {
                a.iter().zip(b).all(|(a, b)| walk(a, b, map))
            }
            _ => a == b,
        }
    }
    let mut map = Default::default();
    walk(
        &serde_json::to_value(child).ok()?,
        &serde_json::to_value(parent).ok()?,
        &mut map,
    )
    .then_some(map)
}

/// The retirement phase shared by activation and its read-only occupancy
/// preview. Selection and the predecessor deadline predicate live here once;
/// checking a candidate must not count a row this phase would remove.
pub(crate) struct Retirement<'a> {
    affected: Vec<(String, &'a Table)>,
    now: i64,
}

impl<'a> Retirement<'a> {
    pub(crate) fn new(next: &Schema, previous: &'a Schema, now: i64) -> Self {
        let affected = next
            .through_order()
            .into_iter()
            .filter_map(|name| {
                let next = &next.tables[&name];
                let old = previous.table(&name)?;
                Self::changes_lifetime(next,old).then_some((name, old))
            })
            .collect();
        Self { affected, now }
    }

    fn changes_lifetime(next:&Table,old:&Table) -> bool {
        next.through != old.through || next.through.is_some() && (next.expire != old.expire || next.expire.as_ref().is_some_and(|field|old.defaults.get(field)!=next.defaults.get(field)))
    }

    pub(crate) fn retires_image(next:&Table,old:&Table,row:&Value,now:i64) -> bool {
        (next.through!=old.through || next.expire!=old.expire || old.expire.as_ref().is_some_and(|field|old.defaults.get(field)!=next.defaults.get(field))) && old.expired(row,now)
    }

    pub(crate) fn affects(&self, name: &str) -> bool {
        self.affected.iter().any(|(table, _)| table == name)
    }

    pub(crate) fn retires(&self, name: &str, row: &Value) -> bool {
        self.affected
            .iter()
            .find(|(table, _)| table == name)
            .is_some_and(|(_, old)| old.expired(row, self.now))
    }
}

//! Private filesystem-owner control transport; never an HTTP application door.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — the host remains the only database writer.
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::{Value, json};
#[cfg(unix)]
use std::os::unix::{
    ffi::OsStrExt,
    fs::{DirBuilderExt, FileTypeExt, MetadataExt, OpenOptionsExt, PermissionsExt},
    net::{UnixListener, UnixStream},
};
use std::{
    fs,
    io::{self, Read, Write},
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};

const INPUT: usize = 4096;
const OUTPUT: usize = 64 * 1024;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "command", rename_all = "kebab-case", deny_unknown_fields)]
pub(crate) enum Command {
    Status {
        after: u64,
        items_after: Option<String>,
        event: Option<u64>,
    },
    Activate {
        release: u64,
        expected_release: Option<u64>,
        expected_revision: u64,
        digest: String,
        rollback: bool,
        restore_dormant: bool,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    },
    Prune {
        confirm: bool,
        expected_revision: u64,
    },
}
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct Envelope {
    identity: String,
    command: Command,
}

#[cfg(unix)]
pub(crate) fn identity(store: &Path) -> Result<String, String> {
    let path = store.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let m = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&path)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
        .ok_or("store disappeared")?;
    Ok(crate::native::digest(
        &serde_json::to_vec(&(path.as_os_str().as_bytes(), m.dev(), m.ino())).unwrap(),
    ))
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn peer(stream: &UnixStream) -> io::Result<()> {
    use std::os::fd::AsRawFd;
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    let uid = {
        let mut credentials = std::mem::MaybeUninit::<libc::ucred>::uninit();
        let mut size = std::mem::size_of::<libc::ucred>() as libc::socklen_t;
        if unsafe {
            libc::getsockopt(
                stream.as_raw_fd(),
                libc::SOL_SOCKET,
                libc::SO_PEERCRED,
                credentials.as_mut_ptr().cast(),
                &mut size,
            )
        } != 0
        {
            return Err(io::Error::last_os_error());
        }
        if size as usize != std::mem::size_of::<libc::ucred>() {
            return Err(io::Error::other("invalid peer credentials"));
        }
        unsafe { credentials.assume_init() }.uid
    };
    #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
    let uid = {
        let (mut uid, mut gid) = (0, 0);
        if unsafe { libc::getpeereid(stream.as_raw_fd(), &mut uid, &mut gid) } != 0 {
            return Err(io::Error::last_os_error());
        }
        uid
    };
    #[cfg(not(any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "macos")))]
    return Err(io::Error::other(
        "release control peer credentials unsupported on this Unix host",
    ));
    #[cfg(any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "macos"))]
    if uid != unsafe { libc::geteuid() } {
        return Err(io::Error::other(
            "release control requires the same filesystem owner",
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn socket_entry(path: &Path) -> io::Result<fs::Metadata> {
    let m = fs::symlink_metadata(path)?;
    if !m.file_type().is_socket()
        || m.uid() != unsafe { libc::geteuid() }
        || m.permissions().mode() & 0o7777 != 0o600
    {
        return Err(io::Error::other(
            "release control must be a same-owner 0600 socket, never a symlink or regular file",
        ));
    }
    Ok(m)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#51-path-independent-control-address — fixed short runtime root, independent of shell environment.
#[cfg(unix)]
fn private_directory(path: &Path, create: bool) -> io::Result<()> {
    if create {
        match fs::DirBuilder::new().mode(0o700).create(path) {
            Ok(()) => {}
            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => {}
            Err(e) => return Err(e),
        }
    }
    // Do not chmod or follow an existing entry. A stranger can pre-create a
    // name in /tmp, but cannot turn that into a capability or chmod target.
    let directory = fs::OpenOptions::new()
        .read(true)
        .custom_flags(libc::O_DIRECTORY | libc::O_NOFOLLOW)
        .open(path)?;
    directory_metadata(&directory.metadata()?, unsafe { libc::geteuid() })
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn directory_metadata(m: &fs::Metadata, uid: u32) -> io::Result<()> {
    if !m.is_dir() || m.uid() != uid || m.permissions().mode() & 0o7777 != 0o700 {
        return Err(io::Error::other(
            "release control runtime directory must be a same-owner 0700 directory",
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn address(dir: &Path, create: bool) -> io::Result<PathBuf> {
    let directory = PathBuf::from(format!("/tmp/snapback4-{}", unsafe { libc::geteuid() }));
    private_directory(&directory, create)?;
    let canonical = dir.canonicalize()?;
    // Bind the address to immutable deployment source, even when two
    // deployments symlink their database to the same store. The actual
    // database path/inode is separately checked in every envelope.
    Ok(directory.join(format!(
        "{}.sock",
        crate::native::digest(canonical.as_os_str().as_bytes())
    )))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#51-path-independent-control-address — a total input deadline bounds shutdown even when a peer keeps sending bytes.
#[cfg(unix)]
fn input(stream: &mut UnixStream, timeout: Duration) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let deadline = Instant::now() + timeout;
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    let mut buffer = [0; INPUT + 1];
    while bytes.len() <= INPUT {
        let remaining = deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now());
        if remaining.is_zero() {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::TimedOut,
                "release control input deadline exceeded",
            ));
        }
        stream.set_read_timeout(Some(remaining))?;
        match stream.read(&mut buffer[..INPUT + 1 - bytes.len()]) {
            Ok(0) => break,
            Ok(n) => bytes.extend_from_slice(&buffer[..n]),
            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::Interrupted => continue,
            Err(e) => return Err(e),
        }
    }
    Ok(bytes)
}

/// Only called while the exclusive database sidecar is held, so stale socket
/// replacement cannot disconnect another admitted serving owner.
#[cfg(unix)]
pub(crate) struct Listener {
    listener: UnixListener,
    path: PathBuf,
    inode: (u64, u64),
    identity: String,
}
#[cfg(unix)]
impl Listener {
    pub(crate) fn bind(dir: &Path, store: &Path) -> Result<Self, String> {
        let identity = identity(store)?;
        let path = address(dir, true).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        match fs::symlink_metadata(&path) {
            Ok(_) => {
                socket_entry(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                match UnixStream::connect(&path) {
                    Ok(_) => {
                        return Err("release control socket already has a live listener".into());
                    }
                    Err(e)
                        if matches!(
                            e.kind(),
                            io::ErrorKind::ConnectionRefused | io::ErrorKind::NotFound
                        ) => {}
                    Err(e) => return Err(e.to_string()),
                }
                fs::remove_file(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {}
            Err(e) => return Err(e.to_string()),
        }
        let listener = UnixListener::bind(&path)
            .map_err(|e| format!("release control {}: {e}", path.display()))?;
        let m = fs::symlink_metadata(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let owned = Self {
            listener,
            path,
            inode: (m.dev(), m.ino()),
            identity,
        };
        // Establish cleanup custody before any fallible setup after bind.
        fs::set_permissions(&owned.path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        owned
            .listener
            .set_nonblocking(true)
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        socket_entry(&owned.path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        Ok(owned)
    }
    fn accept(&self) -> io::Result<UnixStream> {
        let (stream, _) = self.listener.accept()?;
        // Darwin inherits O_NONBLOCK from the listener; input's deadline and
        // socket timeouts require blocking I/O, even before the client writes.
        stream.set_nonblocking(false)?;
        Ok(stream)
    }
    fn handle(
        &self,
        stream: &mut UnixStream,
        mut handle: impl FnMut(Command) -> Result<Value, String>,
    ) {
        let result = (|| -> Result<Value, String> {
            peer(&stream).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            stream
                .set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(5)))
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let bytes = input(stream, Duration::from_secs(5)).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if bytes.len() > INPUT {
                return Err("release control command exceeds 4096 bytes".into());
            }
            let request: Envelope = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if request.identity != self.identity {
                return Err("release control store identity differs; run releases against the owned deployment".into());
            }
            handle(request.command)
        })();
        let reply = match result {
            Ok(value) => json!({"ok":value}),
            Err(error) => json!({"error":error}),
        };
        let mut bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&reply).unwrap();
        if bytes.len() > OUTPUT {
            bytes=serde_json::to_vec(&json!({"error":"release control reply exceeds its bound; inspect paginated `snapback4 releases <dir>`; an admitted activation/prune may already have committed"})).unwrap();
        }
        // A timeout/lost peer does not cancel or undo admitted work.
        let _ = stream.write_all(&bytes);
    }
    pub(crate) fn run(self, mut handle: impl FnMut(Command) -> Result<Value, String>) {
        while !crate::lifecycle::stopping() {
            match self.accept() {
                Ok(mut stream) => {
                    self.handle(&mut stream, &mut handle);
                }
                Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::WouldBlock => {
                    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(25))
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    snapback4_core::diagnostic!("release control accept: {e}");
                    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50));
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
#[cfg(unix)]
impl Drop for Listener {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if fs::symlink_metadata(&self.path)
            .is_ok_and(|m| m.file_type().is_socket() && (m.dev(), m.ino()) == self.inode)
        {
            let _ = fs::remove_file(&self.path);
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(unix)]
pub(crate) fn request(dir: &Path, command: Command) -> Result<Value, String> {
    let store = dir.join("store.sqlite");
    let unavailable = |reason: String| {
        let owner_path = store.canonicalize().unwrap_or_else(|_| store.clone());
        let detail = if reason.contains("No such file or directory") {
            "this deployment has no release control socket".to_owned()
        } else {
            reason
        };
        format!(
            "{}; release control unavailable: {detail}; stop `snapback4 serve {}`, rerun the exact command, then `snapback4 serve {}`",
            crate::store_owner::busy(&owner_path),
            dir.display(),
            dir.display()
        )
    };
    let path = address(dir, false).map_err(|e| unavailable(e.to_string()))?;
    let before = socket_entry(&path).map_err(|e| unavailable(e.to_string()))?;
    let mut stream = UnixStream::connect(&path).map_err(|e| unavailable(e.to_string()))?;
    peer(&stream).map_err(|e| unavailable(e.to_string()))?;
    let after = socket_entry(&path).map_err(|e| unavailable(e.to_string()))?;
    if (before.dev(), before.ino()) != (after.dev(), after.ino()) {
        return Err(unavailable(
            "release control socket changed before send".into(),
        ));
    }
    stream
        .set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(120)))
        .map_err(|e| unavailable(e.to_string()))?;
    stream
        .set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(5)))
        .map_err(|e| unavailable(e.to_string()))?;
    let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&Envelope {
        identity: identity(&store)?,
        command,
    })
    .unwrap();
    if bytes.len() > INPUT {
        return Err("release control command exceeds 4096 bytes".into());
    }
    let ambiguous = |reason: io::Error| {
        format!(
            "release command result is unknown: {reason}; run `snapback4 releases {}` to inspect database authority; the server continues admitted work. Do not automatically deploy another release",
            dir.display()
        )
    };
    stream.write_all(&bytes).map_err(ambiguous)?;
    stream
        .shutdown(std::net::Shutdown::Write)
        .map_err(ambiguous)?;
    let mut reply = Vec::new();
    stream
        .take((OUTPUT + 1) as u64)
        .read_to_end(&mut reply)
        .map_err(ambiguous)?;
    if reply.len() > OUTPUT {
        return Err("release reply too large; inspect releases before retrying".into());
    }
    let value: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&reply).map_err(|e| {
        format!(
            "release result is unknown ({e}); inspect `snapback4 releases {}` before retrying",
            dir.display()
        )
    })?;
    match value.get("ok") {
        Some(value) => Ok(value.clone()),
        None => Err(value["error"]
            .as_str()
            .unwrap_or("release control refused command; inspect releases")
            .into()),
    }
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
pub(crate) fn request(_: &Path, _: Command) -> Result<Value, String> {
    Err("local release control requires Unix".into())
}

#[cfg(all(test, unix))]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::sync::{
        atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering},
        mpsc,
    };
    static NEXT: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
    struct Directory(PathBuf);
    impl Directory {
        fn new(long: bool) -> Self {
            let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
                "sb4-roll-control-{}-{}",
                std::process::id(),
                NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
            ));
            let root = if long {
                root.join("long-path-component".repeat(6))
            } else {
                root
            };
            snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&root).unwrap();
            let _store = snapback4_core::Store::open(&root.join("store.sqlite")).unwrap();
            Self(root)
        }
    }
    impl Drop for Directory {
        fn drop(&mut self) {
            let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn socket_permissions_and_entry_types_are_enforced() {
        let dir = Directory::new(false);
        let path = address(&dir.0, true).unwrap();
        fs::write(&path, "not a socket").unwrap();
        assert!(
            Listener::bind(&dir.0, &dir.0.join("store.sqlite"))
                .err()
                .unwrap()
                .contains("0600 socket")
        );
        fs::remove_file(&path).unwrap();
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink("store.sqlite", &path).unwrap();
        assert!(Listener::bind(&dir.0, &dir.0.join("store.sqlite")).is_err());
        fs::remove_file(&path).unwrap();
        let listener = Listener::bind(&dir.0, &dir.0.join("store.sqlite")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            fs::metadata(&path).unwrap().permissions().mode() & 0o777,
            0o600
        );
        for mode in [0o666, 0o1600] {
            fs::set_permissions(&path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(mode)).unwrap();
            assert!(
                request(
                    &dir.0,
                    Command::Status {
                        after: 0,
                        items_after: None,
                        event: None
                    }
                )
                .unwrap_err()
                .contains("0600 socket")
            );
        }
        drop(listener);
        assert!(!path.exists());
    }
    #[test]
    fn accepted_nonblocking_listener_waits_for_delayed_client_input() {
        let dir = Directory::new(false);
        let listener = Listener::bind(&dir.0, &dir.0.join("store.sqlite")).unwrap();
        let path = listener.path.clone();
        let envelope = Envelope {
            identity: listener.identity.clone(),
            command: Command::Status { after: 0, items_after: None, event: None },
        };
        // Connect before starting accept so the listener stays nonblocking.
        let mut client = UnixStream::connect(&path).unwrap();
        client.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10))).unwrap();
        client.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10))).unwrap();
        let (accepted, waiting) = mpsc::channel();
        let task = std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let mut stream = listener.accept().unwrap();
            accepted.send(()).unwrap();
            listener.handle(&mut stream, |command| {
                assert!(matches!(command, Command::Status { after: 0, .. }));
                Ok(json!({"delayed":true}))
            });
        });
        waiting.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(10)).unwrap();
        // Without normalization Darwin reads WouldBlock and closes the peer
        // here. Linux accept does not inherit O_NONBLOCK, so it cannot expose
        // the unfixed platform bug with this test.
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
        client.write_all(&serde_json::to_vec(&envelope).unwrap()).unwrap();
        client.shutdown(std::net::Shutdown::Write).unwrap();
        let mut reply = String::new();
        client.read_to_string(&mut reply).unwrap();
        task.join().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&reply).unwrap(), json!({"ok":{"delayed":true}}));
    }
    #[test]
    fn input_deadline_expires_while_the_peer_keeps_sending() {
        let (mut reader, mut writer) = UnixStream::pair().unwrap();
        let task = std::thread::spawn(move || {
            while writer.write_all(b" ").is_ok() {
                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
            }
        });
        let started = Instant::now();
        let error = input(&mut reader, Duration::from_millis(300)).unwrap_err();
        assert!(
            matches!(
                error.kind(),
                io::ErrorKind::TimedOut | io::ErrorKind::WouldBlock
            ),
            "{error}"
        );
        assert!(
            started.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(2),
            "input deadline reset on each read"
        );
        drop(reader);
        task.join().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn runtime_directory_refuses_permissions_symlinks_files_and_wrong_owner() {
        let dir = Directory::new(false);
        let runtime = dir.0.join("runtime");
        private_directory(&runtime, true).unwrap();
        private_directory(&runtime, false).unwrap();
        let m = fs::metadata(&runtime).unwrap();
        assert!(directory_metadata(&m, m.uid().wrapping_add(1)).is_err());
        for mode in [0o755, 0o777, 0o1700] {
            fs::set_permissions(&runtime, fs::Permissions::from_mode(mode)).unwrap();
            assert!(private_directory(&runtime, true).is_err());
            assert!(private_directory(&runtime, false).is_err());
            assert_eq!(fs::metadata(&runtime).unwrap().mode() & 0o7777, mode);
        }
        fs::remove_dir(&runtime).unwrap();
        fs::write(&runtime, "untouched").unwrap();
        assert!(private_directory(&runtime, true).is_err());
        assert_eq!(fs::read(&runtime).unwrap(), b"untouched");
        fs::remove_file(&runtime).unwrap();
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&dir.0, &runtime).unwrap();
        assert!(private_directory(&runtime, true).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn stale_socket_is_replaced_but_a_live_listener_is_preserved() {
        let dir = Directory::new(false);
        let store = dir.0.join("store.sqlite");
        let path = address(&dir.0, true).unwrap();
        let stale = UnixListener::bind(&path).unwrap();
        fs::set_permissions(&path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600)).unwrap();
        drop(stale); // Process death leaves the socket entry behind.
        let live = Listener::bind(&dir.0, &store).unwrap();
        let inode = live.inode;
        assert!(
            Listener::bind(&dir.0, &store)
                .err()
                .unwrap()
                .contains("live listener")
        );
        let m = fs::symlink_metadata(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((m.dev(), m.ino()), inode);
        drop(live);
        assert!(!path.exists());
    }

    #[test]
    fn cleanup_preserves_a_replacement_and_canonical_aliases_share_an_address() {
        let dir = Directory::new(false);
        let other = Directory::new(false);
        let store = dir.0.join("store.sqlite");
        let listener = Listener::bind(&dir.0, &store).unwrap();
        let path = listener.path.clone();
        assert_ne!(path, address(&other.0, true).unwrap());
        let alias = dir.0.join("alias");
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&dir.0, &alias).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(path, address(&alias, false).unwrap());
        assert!(path.as_os_str().as_bytes().len() < 104);
        fs::remove_file(&path).unwrap();
        fs::write(&path, "replacement").unwrap();
        drop(listener);
        assert_eq!(fs::read(&path).unwrap(), b"replacement");
        fs::remove_file(&path).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejected_store_identity_does_not_execute_command() {
        let dir = Directory::new(false);
        let listener = Listener::bind(&dir.0, &dir.0.join("store.sqlite")).unwrap();
        listener.listener.set_nonblocking(false).unwrap();
        let task = std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let (mut stream, _) = listener.listener.accept().unwrap();
            listener.handle(&mut stream, |_| panic!("wrong identity admitted"));
        });
        let mut stream = UnixStream::connect(address(&dir.0, true).unwrap()).unwrap();
        stream.write_all(br#"{"identity":"wrong","command":{"command":"status","after":0,"items_after":null,"event":null}}"#).unwrap();
        stream.shutdown(std::net::Shutdown::Write).unwrap();
        let mut reply = String::new();
        stream.read_to_string(&mut reply).unwrap();
        assert!(reply.contains("identity differs"));
        task.join().unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn lost_reply_does_not_cancel_admitted_command() {
        let dir = Directory::new(false);
        let listener = Listener::bind(&dir.0, &dir.0.join("store.sqlite")).unwrap();
        listener.listener.set_nonblocking(false).unwrap();
        let (entered, waiting) = mpsc::channel();
        let (resume, resumed) = mpsc::channel();
        let (done, finished) = mpsc::channel();
        let task = std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let (mut stream, _) = listener.listener.accept().unwrap();
            listener.handle(&mut stream, |_| {
                entered.send(()).unwrap();
                resumed.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(10)).unwrap();
                done.send(()).unwrap();
                Ok(json!({"committed":true}))
            });
        });
        let mut stream = UnixStream::connect(address(&dir.0, true).unwrap()).unwrap();
        let envelope = Envelope {
            identity: identity(&dir.0.join("store.sqlite")).unwrap(),
            command: Command::Prune {
                confirm: true,
                expected_revision: 1,
            },
        };
        stream
            .write_all(&serde_json::to_vec(&envelope).unwrap())
            .unwrap();
        stream.shutdown(std::net::Shutdown::Write).unwrap();
        waiting.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(10)).unwrap();
        drop(stream);
        resume.send(()).unwrap();
        finished.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(10)).unwrap();
        task.join().unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn long_deployment_path_uses_same_private_socket() {
        let dir = Directory::new(true);
        assert!(dir.0.as_os_str().as_bytes().len() > 108);
        let listener = Listener::bind(&dir.0, &dir.0.join("store.sqlite")).unwrap();
        listener.listener.set_nonblocking(false).unwrap();
        let task = std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let (mut stream, _) = listener.listener.accept().unwrap();
            listener.handle(&mut stream, |_| Ok(json!({"release":2})));
        });
        assert_eq!(
            request(
                &dir.0,
                Command::Status {
                    after: 0,
                    items_after: None,
                    event: None
                }
            )
            .unwrap()["release"],
            2
        );
        task.join().unwrap();
        assert!(!address(&dir.0, true).unwrap().exists());
    }
}

//! Ownership locks the database's sidecar; stat pins its inode. `serve.lock` is only an address hint.
use std::{fs::{self, File}, io::{Read, Seek, SeekFrom, Write}, path::{Path, PathBuf}, time::Duration};
use snapback4_core::{Engine, RuntimeError, Store};
#[cfg(unix)]
use std::os::unix::fs::MetadataExt;

pub(crate) struct Owner {
    _sidecar: std::sync::Arc<File>,
    identity: (u64, u64),
    path: PathBuf,
    record: Option<File>,
    reclaimed: Option<String>,
    pub(crate) token_authority: Option<crate::serve::token::Authority>,
}

/// The address `doctor` dials for the server that owns this store: the
/// published loopback address itself, or, for a wildcard bind, that family's
/// loopback on the same port. A published non-loopback address is not followed.
pub(crate) fn inspector_address(store: &Path) -> Option<std::net::SocketAddr> {
    let path = store.canonicalize().ok()?;
    dialable(&address(&path)?)
}

fn dialable(published: &str) -> Option<std::net::SocketAddr> {
    use std::net::{IpAddr, Ipv4Addr, Ipv6Addr, SocketAddr};
    let address = published.strip_prefix("http://")?.parse::<SocketAddr>().ok()?;
    let ip = match address.ip() {
        ip if ip.is_loopback() => ip,
        IpAddr::V4(ip) if ip.is_unspecified() => IpAddr::V4(Ipv4Addr::LOCALHOST),
        IpAddr::V6(ip) if ip.is_unspecified() => IpAddr::V6(Ipv6Addr::LOCALHOST),
        _ => return None,
    };
    (address.port() != 0).then(|| SocketAddr::new(ip, address.port()))
}

fn resolve(store: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    match store.canonicalize() {
        Ok(path) => Ok(path),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
            let parent = store.parent().filter(|p| !p.as_os_str().is_empty()).unwrap_or(Path::new("."));
            snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(parent).map_err(|e| failure(store, e))?;
            Ok(parent.canonicalize().map_err(|e| failure(store, e))?.join(store.file_name().ok_or("store path has no filename")?))
        }
        Err(e) => Err(failure(store, e)),
    }
}

fn failure(path: &Path, reason: impl std::fmt::Display) -> String {
    format!("E_STORE at {}: {reason}; restore a regular store/address file and retry", path.display())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn same_file(a: &fs::Metadata, b: &fs::Metadata) -> bool { a.dev() == b.dev() && a.ino() == b.ino() }

#[cfg(unix)]
fn regular(path: &Path, create: bool, database: &Path) -> Result<File, String> {
    snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(database, path, create, 128)
        .map_err(|e| failure(path, e))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — sidecar flocks fence backend writers and share device ownership without locking SQLite.
pub(crate) fn command(store: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> { acquire(store, false, Duration::ZERO) }
pub(crate) fn device(store: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> { acquire(store, true, Duration::ZERO) }
// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.3 — a restarting host waits for watchdogs to finish group cleanup.
pub(crate) fn host(store: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> { acquire(store, false, Duration::from_secs(5)) }

#[cfg(unix)]
fn acquire(store: &Path, shared: bool, wait: Duration) -> Result<Owner, String> {
    let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + wait;
    for _ in 0..16 {
        let path = resolve(store)?;
        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(path.parent().ok_or("store path has no parent")?)
            .map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?;
        // Stat before opening either auxiliary file; a refused probe creates
        // no database. Only the sidecar lock grants permission to create one.
        let before = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&path).map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?;
        let sidecar = snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_file(&path)
            .map_err(|e| failure(&snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_path(&path), e))?;
        let mut cleanup = None;
        loop {
            let locked = if shared { sidecar.try_lock_shared() } else { sidecar.try_lock() };
            match locked {
                Ok(()) => break,
                Err(fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock) if std::time::Instant::now() < deadline && *cleanup.get_or_insert_with(|| stopped_host(&path)) => std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(25)),
                Err(fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock) => return Err(busy(&path)),
                Err(fs::TryLockError::Error(error)) => return Err(failure(&path, error)),
            }
        }
        if !snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_is_named(&path, &sidecar) { continue; }
        snapback4_core::private_fs::database_file(&path).map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?;
        let database = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&path).map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?
            .ok_or_else(|| failure(&path, "database disappeared during ownership acquisition"))?;
        if before.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| !same_file(old, &database)) { continue; }
        let mut reclaimed = None;
        let record = if shared { None } else {
            let record_path = path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
            if let Some(pid)=owner_pid(&path) {
                if unsafe {libc::kill(pid as libc::pid_t,0)} == -1 && std::io::Error::last_os_error().raw_os_error()==Some(libc::ESRCH) {
                    reclaimed = Some(format!("reclaimed stale serve.lock: dead PID {pid}; directory {}", path.parent().unwrap().display()));
                }
            }
            let file = regular(&record_path, true, &path)?;
            file.set_len(0).map_err(|e| failure(&record_path, e))?;
            Some(file)
        };
        return Ok(Owner { _sidecar: std::sync::Arc::new(sidecar), identity: (database.dev(), database.ino()), path, record, reclaimed, token_authority: None });
    }
    Err(failure(store, "database path kept changing during ownership acquisition"))
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn acquire(store: &Path, _: bool, _: Duration) -> Result<Owner, String> { Err(failure(store, "database ownership requires Unix")) }

pub(crate) fn stopped_host(path: &Path) -> bool {
    // A CLI owner or a host not yet announced still refuses immediately. A
    // reachable listener does too; only a prior host's cleanup gets a wait.
    // This is a refusal/wait hint, never permission to open the store: the
    // sidecar must still be acquired. Never dial a non-loopback address hint.
    address(path).is_some_and(|published| dialable(&published).is_none_or(|address|
        std::net::TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address, Duration::from_millis(50)).is_err()))
}

pub(crate) fn busy(path: &Path) -> String {
    let owner = address(path).map(|address| match owner_pid(path) { Some(pid) => format!("{address} (pid {pid})"), None => address })
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "another CLI command or a server starting up (address pending)".into());
    format!("E_STORE_BUSY at {}: this store is live; owned by {owner}; directory {}; no work was admitted. Stop its `snapback4 dev` or `snapback4 serve` process (or wait for the owning CLI command), rerun this command once, then restart the server. Exclusion holds while <database>-owner is left in place; deleting or replacing it while a process runs ends exclusion and does not stop that process", path.display(),path.parent().unwrap_or(Path::new(".")).display())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
pub(crate) fn address(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
    // A missing/replaced address record cannot grant ownership. Never follow
    // a symlink (including one to the database), nor block on a FIFO.
    let record = path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
    let file = regular(&record, false, path).ok()?;
    let mut text = String::new();
    file.take(1024).read_to_string(&mut text).ok()?;
    let mut lines = text.lines();
    let port = lines.next().and_then(|s| s.parse::<u16>().ok());
    lines.next().map(str::to_owned).or_else(|| port.map(|p| format!("http://127.0.0.1:{p}")))
}

/// The owning host's PID from its address record (`pid N` on the third line).
#[cfg(unix)]
fn owner_pid(path: &Path) -> Option<u32> {
    let record = path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
    let file = regular(&record, false, path).ok()?;
    let mut text = String::new();
    file.take(1024).read_to_string(&mut text).ok()?;
    text.lines().nth(2)?.strip_prefix("pid ")?.parse().ok()
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn owner_pid(_: &Path) -> Option<u32> { None }

#[cfg(not(unix))]
pub(crate) fn address(_: &Path) -> Option<String> { None }

impl Owner {
    pub(crate) fn report_reclaim(&self, verbose: bool) {
        if verbose && let Some(note) = &self.reclaimed { eprintln!("note: {note}"); }
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — unlink only our address inode; the ownership sidecar remains named.
    pub(crate) fn clear_address(&self) {
        #[cfg(unix)]
        if let Some(file) = &self.record {
            let path = self.path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
            if let (Ok(held), Ok(named)) = (file.metadata(), fs::symlink_metadata(&path))
                && same_file(&held, &named) {
                let _ = fs::remove_file(path);
            }
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn worker_lock(&self) -> std::sync::Arc<File> { self._sidecar.clone() }

    pub(crate) fn path(&self) -> Result<&Path, RuntimeError> {
        #[cfg(unix)]
        {
            let meta = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&self.path)
                .map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(failure(&self.path, e)))?;
